# Client behavior sources

Where each client behavior the input, menu and dropdown fakes simulate comes from. A rule is `confirmed` when
Blizzard's Lua shows it; the source column then gives `path:line` relative to `mirror/gethe/<branch>/` under the
testkit home (fetched by `tools/fetch.sh`). A rule is `assumed` when no Lua shows it; the source column then names
the warcraft.wiki.gg page that supports it, or says "no source". Every `assumed` rule has a test whose name ends in
`_assumed`. Rows describe the client. The kit follows them except where a verdict says "kit choice" (M13).

Every Blizzard `path:line` is for these Gethe/wow-ui-source commits, the ones the mirror held when the rows were checked
(each mirror's `COMMIT` file, written by `tools/fetch.sh`). A later fetch moves a mirror to its branch's newest
commit, so lines may drift there; read a cited line at its pinned commit.

| Branch | Commit | Client |
|---|---|---|
| `live` | `b9be9379719047977fca4dba1bb1d9988e8840b3` | 12.1.5.70448 |
| `forever` | `172a387e3ab85b26e6640d3058d5ec1e6ccd4c63` | 1.60.1.70450 |
| `classic` | `15c8a754451ce09a688be11ffa03c064e4973c14` | 5.5.4.70469 |
| `classic_era` | `8165d4cd6e48d606369336cc3a7977902310e81e` | 1.15.9.70003 |
| `classic_anniversary` | `2cfec7c00f0f24762d5ca35a4beb1e93c0cde328` | 2.5.6.70006 |

The kit has these known gaps, which may not be the only ones:

- S5: an `AddButton` to a hidden list that is not being built is dropped; the client appends it there, where only
  `numButtons` sees it until that list's next Initialize or Toggle clears it (`framexml/fakes/dropdown.lua`).
- S1, S16: the client indexes `button` past level 1 (Mainline `:1168`, Classic `:1107`) and, with no frame, at
  `:1065`/`:1004`, so a Toggle without one errors; the kit accepts a nil `button` (`framexml/fakes/dropdown.lua`).
- S16: `s:menu()` and `s:pick` start at level 1, so they report no menu while a list is shown above a closed level 1,
  and `s:menu()` stops at the first closed level, so open levels 1 and 3 read as level 1 alone (`scenario_menu.lua`).
- S17: `AddButton` does not check the entry from the frame's selected name, ID or value (Mainline `:633-648`, Classic
  `:568-583`), and `UIDropDownMenu_SetSelectedValue` stores the value without the client's `UIDropDownMenu_Refresh`
  (`framexml/fakes/dropdown.lua`).
- S14: the layout globals (`UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXBUTTONS`, `_BUTTON_HEIGHT`, `_BORDER_HEIGHT`, `_SHOW_TIME`,
  `_DEFAULT_*`) and `OPEN_DROPDOWNMENUS` are not set (`framexml/fakes/dropdown.lua`).
- M4: a `SetEnabled` function is re-read on (re)initialize and before a pick, never on the client's 0.2 s poll.
- M10: `Menu.ModifyMenu` does not run a new hook at once on an already generated menu with that tag
  (`framexml/fakes/menu.lua`).
- D1: a held Shift is not read, since the kit has no key state (`framexml/fakes/menu_dropdown.lua`).
- D9: a `DropdownButton` fires no `OnUpdate`/`OnMenuOpen`/`OnMenuClose` callback events and has no
  `RegisterCallback` (`framexml/fakes/menu_dropdown.lua`).

## Input steps

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg (`https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/<page>`), read 2026-10-20. Blizzard paths are
relative to `mirror/gethe/<branch>/`. No Lua in the mirror sets or reads the rules below beyond the cited lines
(`Blizzard_Menu` and `UIDropDownMenu.lua` are the only checked-out UI Lua).

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| I1 Button `Enable`/`Disable` fire `OnEnable`/`OnDisable` only when the state changes | assumed | no source: Widget_script_handlers says only "OnEnable(self) - Invoked when the frame is enabled."; `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/MenuTemplates.lua:127-128` sets the handlers but shows no change guard |
| I2 `RegisterForClicks` default `"LeftButtonUp"`; accepts `"AnyUp"`, `"AnyDown"`, `"<Button>Up"`, `"<Button>Down"` | confirmed | wiki `API_Button_RegisterForClicks`: "The default button registered on new button objects is `LeftButtonUp`." and the value list `AnyUp`, `AnyDown`, `LeftButtonUp`/`Down`, `RightButtonUp`/`Down`, `MiddleButtonUp`/`Down`, `Button4Up`/`Down`, `Button5Up`/`Down`; call forms in `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_SharedXML/Mainline/UIDropDownMenu.lua:452-456` |
| I3 `Button:Click(button?, isDown?)` on an enabled button runs `PreClick`, `OnClick`, `PostClick` with `(self, button or "LeftButton", isDown)` (`isDown` default `false`), ignoring `RegisterForClicks`; on a disabled one, or one whose click scripts are running, it does nothing. Kit choices: it skips mouse and visibility checks, and a non-boolean `isDown` reaches the scripts as a boolean | confirmed (scripts, arguments, defaults, registration, disabled and re-entry); assumed (kit choice: no mouse or visibility check, `isDown` coerced to a boolean) | wiki `API_Button_Click`: "The arguments to this function will be passed to the widget's PreClick, OnClick, and PostClick script handlers.", `button` default "= LeftButton", "This function ignores the widget's Button:RegisterForClicks preferences", "does not fire the OnMouseDown and OnMouseUp script handlers", "This function does nothing if the Button widget is disabled, or if the button is already being clicked." `isDown`: `api/retail/SimpleButtonAPI.lua:35-40` (same in every flavor) gives `default = false`, `lua = "boolean"`. Assumed, no source: the wiki is silent on mouse and visibility checks (only "a virtual mouse click"), and nothing says what the scripts get for a non-boolean `isDown` |
| I4 a CheckButton flips its checked state before `PreClick` | assumed | no source: wiki `UIOBJECT_CheckButton` says only "Interacting with anything within the HitRectInsets toggles the button state" and `API_CheckButton_SetChecked` "Does not fire OnClick"; neither gives the order of the flip |
| I5 mouse defaults: Button, CheckButton, EditBox start mouse-enabled; setting `OnEnter`/`OnLeave`/`OnMouseDown`/`OnMouseUp` implies `EnableMouse(true)` (both) | assumed (auto-enable: wiki `API_ScriptRegion_EnableMouse`; per-type defaults: no source) | wiki `API_ScriptRegion_EnableMouse`: "Setting the OnEnter/Leave, OnMouseDown/Up script automatically implies `EnableMouse(true)`" and "EnableMouse implies SetMouseMotionEnabled and SetMouseClickEnabled", so one script enables both (the wiki text may predate the click/motion split). Defaults for Button, CheckButton, EditBox unsettled: the wiki only gives a fresh Frame as mouse-disabled; `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/MenuTemplates.lua:597-600` enables both on a pooled Button explicitly |
| I6 EditBox `SetText` fires `OnTextChanged(self, false)`, and whether `OnTextSet` fires too (and in which order); a `SetText` that keeps the text fires no `OnTextChanged` | confirmed; `OnTextSet` for unchanged text: assumed | wiki `UIHANDLER_OnTextSet`: "Fires when setting text programmatically." and "Fires before OnTextChanged"; `UIHANDLER_OnTextChanged`: "True when changing as a result of user input, false when programmatically set." Verdict: `SetText` fires `OnTextSet(self)` first, then `OnTextChanged(self, false)`. The `API_EditBox_SetText` page itself names no scripts. Unchanged text: Blizzard's `ColorPickerHexBoxMixin:OnTextChanged` calls `self:SetText(string.gsub(text, "[^A-Fa-f0-9]", ""))` on every change (`Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_ColorPickerFrame/Mainline/ColorPickerFrame.lua:125-129` on `live`; not in the sparse mirror, read on GitHub at the pinned commit), which would recurse forever if an unchanged `SetText` fired `OnTextChanged`; wowprogramming.com `docs/scripts/OnTextChanged` states the rule: fired by `:SetText()` "(but only if the text is actually changed)". The ColorPicker code fits a client re-entry guard as well, so the change check itself rests on wowprogramming. No page says whether `OnTextSet` fires for unchanged text; the kit fires it, as the wiki gives no exception |
| I7 a click fires `OnMouseDown(self, button)` then `OnMouseUp(self, button, true)` | confirmed | wiki `UIHANDLER_OnClick`: "Preceeded by OnMouseDown, OnMouseUp and PreClick; and followed by PostClick." Signatures from `Widget_script_handlers`: "OnMouseDown(self, button)", "OnMouseUp(self, button, upInside)"; `UIHANDLER_OnMouseUp`: `upInside` is "Whether the cursor was over the frame at moment of the input received" (true for a click). The wiki gives no order for a Down-registered `OnClick` between the two |
| I8 typing a character fires `OnChar(self, char)` then `OnTextChanged(self, true)` | confirmed | wiki `UIHANDLER_OnChar`: signature "(self, text)", text is "The UTF-8 character.", "Fires before OnTextChanged."; `UIHANDLER_OnTextChanged`: "True when changing as a result of user input". Per character only for typing: "When pasting text, this script fires once for each character pasted (OnTextChanged will only fire once at the end)." |
| I9 `GetMouseFoci()` returns `{}` when nothing has the mouse | confirmed | wiki `API_GetMouseFoci`: "This function can return an empty table in cases where the mouse is off-screen, or is over the WorldFrame without first enabling it for mouse motion events."; returns a table, never nil: `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_APIDocumentationGenerated/InputDocumentation.lua:54-60` (live; `classic_era` line 30), `Nilable = false` |
| I10 over the 3D world the focus is `WorldFrame` only when its mouse motion is enabled, else none (`GetMouseFoci()` → `{}`) (`s:hover`) | confirmed | wiki `API_GetMouseFoci`: "This function can return an empty table in cases where the mouse is off-screen, or is over the WorldFrame without first enabling it for mouse motion events." (over the world the new API gives `{}` unless the addon enabled motion on `WorldFrame`). Only the removed `GetMouseFocus` is documented to return it: wiki `API_GetMouseFocus` example `/dump GetMouseFocus():GetName() -- WorldFrame`. `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/Menu.lua:67-72` guards `foci[1]` against nil |
| I11 the mouse focus is already the frame while its `OnEnter` runs and already cleared while its `OnLeave` runs | assumed | no source: no wiki page (`UIHANDLER_OnEnter`, `UIHANDLER_OnLeave`, `API_ScriptRegion_IsMouseOver`) says when focus changes relative to the handler, and no `Blizzard_Menu` handler reads the focus inside `OnEnter`/`OnLeave` (`MenuTemplates.lua:173` reads `IsMouseMotionFocus` at init, not in a handler) |
| I12 a fresh EditBox's `GetText()` returns `""`, never nil | confirmed | the doc marks EditBox `GetText`'s `text` return `nilable = false`, type `cstring` (`api/retail/SimpleEditBoxAPI.lua:361-368`; `forever` `:361`, `mists`, `tbc`, `vanilla` `:360`). wiki `API_EditBox_GetText`: returns "string - The current text"; it does not name the empty case |
| I13 an error in an addon's `PreClick` goes to the error handler once, and `OnClick` and `PostClick` still run (the click scripts of I3) | assumed (user decision, 2026-10-20) | no source: wiki `UIHANDLER_PreClick` ("Fires immediately before OnClick") and `API_Button_Click` do not say what an erroring `PreClick` does to the scripts after it. The kit runs each click script as its own handler call, as it does every script, so one script's error does not stop the next |

## UIDropDownMenu

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg, read 2026-10-20. Blizzard paths are relative to `mirror/gethe/<branch>/`. Two
groups of `UIDropDownMenu.lua`: Mainline (`live`, `forever`; `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_SharedXML/Mainline/`, same
lines up to 1281, `forever` adds a `SmartNavigation` call there) and Classic (`classic`, `classic_era`,
`classic_anniversary`; `.../Classic/`, same lines, `classic_anniversary` differs by line endings only). The mirror holds
no `.xml`, so `UIDropDownMenu.xml` (the `UIDropDownListTemplate` and `DropDownList1..` definitions) cannot be read.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| S1 `ToggleDropDownMenu(level, ...)` when `DropDownList<level>` is shown and `UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU` is the dropdown frame (or the button's parent when no frame is passed) closes that list and deeper instead of reopening | confirmed | Mainline `UIDropDownMenu.lua:1064-1071`, Classic `:1003-1010`: `if listFrame:IsShown() and UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU == tempFrame then listFrame:Hide(); return false`. The test is on the list level given, not only level 1. `tempFrame` is `dropDownFrame`, or `button:GetParent()` when `dropDownFrame` is nil; the check runs before anything indexes a nil `dropDownFrame`, so a frameless level-1 Toggle with a button on the open menu closes it. A submenu entry's own Toggle passes no frame and the entry as `button` (S8), so `tempFrame` is the entry's list, never the open menu. Hiding the list closes deeper lists through `UIDropDownMenu_OnHide` (S12) |
| S2 `info` → kind: `isTitle` → title; `notCheckable` → button; `isNotRadio` → checkbox; else radio; a divider is an `info` with `isTitle` and no text | confirmed | The client has no kinds, only these flags in `UIDropDownMenu_AddButton`: Mainline `:466-469` (`isTitle` → disabled, title font; it does not imply `notCheckable`), `:607`/`:650`/`:704-706` (`notCheckable` → no check marks), `:676-685` (`isNotRadio` → checkbox texture, else radio texture, only when checkable); Classic `:417`, `:542`/`:585`, `:627`. Divider: `UIDropDownMenu_AddSeparator` builds `{isTitle = true, isUninteractable = true, notCheckable = true, iconOnly = true, icon = ...}` with no `text` (Mainline `:367-395`, Classic `:326-354`); a nil `text` sets `""` (Mainline `:521-523`) |
| S3 a closed level's focused frame gets `OnLeave(self, false)` and focus clears | assumed (`OnLeave` on hide: source; `motion = false`: inferred from the wiki's `motion` definition, no source passes it) | Hiding a shown frame under the cursor fires its `OnLeave`: Blizzard comments `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/Menu.lua:1077` and `:1714-1715` (`live`, `forever` `:1716`; Classic branches `:1065`, `:1702`): "this frame should have already been explicitly hidden by the menu system during close, causing the OnLeave to be called if it were necessary", "Hide is necessary here to ensure any OnLeave scripts are fired on child frames". Argument `false`: wiki `UIHANDLER_OnLeave` signature `OnLeave(self, motion)`, `motion` "True if the handler is being run due to actual mouse movement"; a hide is not mouse movement. The wiki does not say what a hide passes (its `false` clause is the copied "cursor entered the frame due to other circumstances"). Clearing the focus is the kit's own model |
| S4 `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize` stores `initialize`, `displayMode`, `menuList` on the frame and calls `init(frame, level, menuList)` at once, with `level` as passed (defaulted to 1 only afterwards); `AddButton` calls made then open nothing; an error in `init` reaches the caller | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize`: Mainline `:77-99`, Classic `:76-97`. Order: `frame.menuList = menuList`; `UIDropDownMenu_InitializeHelper` (Mainline `:41-65`, Classic `:42-66`) hides lists: when `frame` is not `UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU` it resets `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MENU_LEVEL` to 1 (Mainline `:43-45`, Classic `:44-46`) and hides every list; when it is, it hides only the lists `i >= UIDROPDOWNMENU_MENU_LEVEL` (Mainline `:54`, Classic `:55`), the level the last `ToggleDropDownMenu` set (Mainline `:1056`, Classic `:997`), so an Initialize then Toggle(1) on the open menu's frame hides list 1 and the Toggle reopens it. Hiding list 1 clears `UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU` (S6); `UIDropDownMenu_SetInitializeFunction` stores `frame.initialize` (only when `init` is truthy, S19); `initFunction(frame, level, frame.menuList)` is called directly, no `pcall` (Mainline `:83-86`, Classic `:82-85`), so an error propagates. **`level` is passed as given, nil when the caller gave none**; it is defaulted to 1 only after the call (Mainline `:89-91`, Classic `:88-90`). `ToggleDropDownMenu` always passes a number (Mainline `:1193`, Classic `:1132`: `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize(dropDownFrame, dropDownFrame.initialize, nil, level, menuList)`). `displayMode` is stored as `frame.displayMode` only when it is `"MENU"` (`UIDropDownMenu_SetDisplayMode`: Mainline `:105-125`, Classic `:103-122`). Buttons added during a direct Initialize land in `DropDownList<level>` whose list `InitializeHelper` just hid (Mainline `:41-65`; a list below the menu level of the open menu's frame stays shown, S5), so nothing opens; `ToggleDropDownMenu` shows the list after `init` (`:1202`). The helper hides only `DropDownList1..UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS` (Mainline `:51-63`, Classic `:52-64`), so an open Menu API menu stays open |
| S5 `AddButton` with no Initialize or Toggle running is not ignored | confirmed | The guard `if not UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU then return end` is commented out ("Might to uncomment this if there are performance issues"): Mainline `UIDropDownMenu.lua:415-420`, Classic `:374-379`. `AddButton` always appends to `DropDownList<level or 1>` (index `numButtons + 1`, Mainline `:421-439`, Classic `:380-386`) and sets `numButtons`; the list stays hidden unless a menu is open, in which case the button joins the shown list |
| S6 `ToggleDropDownMenu` with `frame = nil` uses the dropdown frame that owns the open level 1 and its stored `initialize` | confirmed | At level 2 and deeper: `if not dropDownFrame then dropDownFrame = UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU end` (Mainline `:1163-1165`, Classic `:1102-1104`), then `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize(dropDownFrame, dropDownFrame.initialize, nil, level, menuList)` (Mainline `:1193`, Classic `:1132`). `UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU` is set by the level-1 Toggle through the delegate attribute `openmenu` (Mainline `:34-35`, `:1095`) and cleared when list 1 hides (`UIDropDownMenu_OnHide`: Mainline `:1347-1349`, Classic `:1249-1251`). The call needs the 8th argument `button` when `frame` is nil: `button:GetParent()` (Mainline `:1064-1065`, Classic `:1003-1004`) errors on nil. At level 1 a nil `frame` errors at `dropDownFrame.xOffset` (Mainline `:1100`) |
| S7 (Mainline: live, forever) a pick closes the entry's level and deeper unless `keepShownOnClick`, then calls `func(self, arg1, arg2, checked, mouseButton)` | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenuButton_OnClick(self, mouseButton)` `:999-1042`. **Close runs before `func`**: `self:GetParent():Hide()` at `:1019` (when not `keepShownOnClick`), `func(self, self.arg1, self.arg2, checked, mouseButton)` at `:1034`; **five arguments**, the fifth is the mouse button. Only the entry's own list hides (`:1019`); `UIDropDownMenu_OnHide` closes deeper levels only, `CloseDropDownMenus(id + 1)` (`live :1345`, `forever :1348`), so a level-2 pick leaves level 1 shown. `checked`: `self.checked`, called with `self` when a function (`:1000-1003`), unprotected and before the close, its value passed to `func` as is (an error ends the click: no close, no `func`); with `keepShownOnClick` and a checkable entry it is flipped (`:1006-1017`); stored back unless it is a function (`:1022-1024`) |
| S7 (Classic: classic, classic_era, classic_anniversary) same order, four arguments: `func(self, arg1, arg2, checked)` | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenuButton_OnClick(self)` `:940-983`. Same order: close `:960`, then `func(self, self.arg1, self.arg2, checked)` `:975`, **four arguments**. Deeper-only close in `UIDropDownMenu_OnHide` `:1247`. `checked` as Mainline (`:941-944`, `:947-958`, `:963-965`) |
| S8 entering an entry with `hasArrow` calls `ToggleDropDownMenu(level + 1, info.value, nil, nil, nil, nil, info.menuList)` | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenuButton_OnEnter` Mainline `:182-191`: `ToggleDropDownMenu(self:GetParent():GetID() + 1, self.value, nil, nil, nil, nil, self.menuList, self, nil, self.menuListDisplayMode)`; Classic `:209-218`: same through `self.menuList, self` (8th argument `button = self`, no 10th). Only `if not listFrame or not listFrame:IsShown() or select(2, listFrame:GetPoint(1)) ~= self` (the level above is not already open for this entry); otherwise nothing. An entry without `hasArrow` calls `CloseDropDownMenus(level + 1)` (Mainline `:190`, Classic `:217`). `self.value` is `info.value`, or `info.text` when `info.value` is nil (Mainline `:584-590` tests `info.value ~= nil`, Classic `:520-526` tests `info.value` truthy) |
| S9 `DropDownList1..3` are Buttons (template `UIDropDownListTemplate`), not Frames; object type per branch | assumed | `live`: Button, `forever`: Button, `classic`: Button, `classic_era`: Button, `classic_anniversary`: Button. Lists made in Lua are `CreateFrame("Button", "DropDownList"..UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS, nil, "UIDropDownListTemplate")` (Mainline `UIDropDownMenu.lua:345`, Classic `:304`), entries `CreateFrame("Button", ..., "UIDropDownMenuButtonTemplate")` (`:353`/`:361`, `:312`/`:320`); the shipped lists 1..`UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS` come from `UIDropDownMenu.xml`, which the mirror does not hold, so their type is inferred from the same template. `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS` starts at 3 on Mainline (`:4`) and 2 on Classic (`:5`): on Classic `DropDownList3` does not exist until `UIDropDownMenu_CreateFrames` makes it |
| S10 `ToggleDropDownMenu` whose `init` adds no entries shows nothing; an entry with `hasArrow` whose submenu `init` adds nothing opens no submenu | confirmed | After `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize(dropDownFrame, dropDownFrame.initialize, nil, level, menuList)`: `if listFrame.numButtons == 0 then return false end` (Mainline `UIDropDownMenu.lua:1193-1197`, Classic `:1132-1136`), before `listFrame:Show()` (Mainline `:1202`). The `hasArrow` enter goes through the same `ToggleDropDownMenu` (S8). The target list was already hidden (S12) |
| S11 entering a disabled entry, `hasArrow` or not, closes the levels below it and opens no submenu | confirmed | A disabled entry's Button is `Disable()`d and its `InvisibleButton` shown over it (Mainline `:471-475`, Classic `:423-426`); `UIDropDownMenuButtonInvisibleButton_OnEnter` only calls `CloseDropDownMenus(self:GetParent():GetParent():GetID() + 1)` (Mainline `:153-154`; Classic `:180-182`, after `UIDropDownMenu_StopCounting`). `UIDropDownMenuButton_OnEnter`, which opens submenus (S8), is the disabled Button's own script and does not run. Titles, and so dividers (S2), are disabled the same way (Mainline `:466-469`, Classic `:417-420`, S13): entering one closes the levels below it too |
| S12 `ToggleDropDownMenu` that does not close (S1) hides the target list and deeper before `init` runs, so an `init` error leaves them hidden | confirmed | After the S1 check: `listFrame:Hide()` "Hide the listframe anyways since it is redrawn OnShow()" (Mainline `:1086-1087`, Classic `:1025-1026`); `UIDropDownMenu_OnHide` closes the deeper lists (`CloseDropDownMenus(id + 1)`, Mainline `:1345`, Classic `:1247`). `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize` then runs `init` unprotected (S4) and `listFrame:Show()` comes after it (Mainline `:1202`), so an error in `init` leaves the list hidden |
| S13 `notClickable` and `isTitle` disable the entry: it cannot be picked | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenu_AddButton` sets `info.disabled = true` for `notClickable` (Mainline `:460-463`, Classic `:411-414`, white font) and for `isTitle` (Mainline `:466-469`, Classic `:417-420`); `info.disabled` then runs `button:Disable()` and shows the `InvisibleButton` (Mainline `:472-476`, Classic `:423-427`). These writes go into the caller's table, so an `info` reused after a title stays disabled; a disabled entry also gets `info.colorCode = nil`, and `info.disablecolor` replaces `colorCode` (Mainline `:475`, `:479-481`; Classic `:426`, `:430-432`) |
| S14 the state globals: `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MENU_LEVEL` starts at 1; `ToggleDropDownMenu` sets `_MENU_LEVEL` and `_MENU_VALUE` before init; a level-1 Toggle that does not close sets `_OPEN_MENU` to its frame; the Initialize helper sets `_INIT_MENU` and resets `_MENU_LEVEL` to 1 for a frame that is not the open menu; list 1's `OnHide` clears `_OPEN_MENU`. Kit choice: the layout globals are not set | confirmed (kit choice: layout globals) | Start values: Mainline `UIDropDownMenu.lua:8-14`, Classic `:9-15`. Toggle: Mainline `:1056-1057`, Classic `:997-998`; `SetAttribute("openmenu", dropDownFrame)` at level 1 (Mainline `:1095`, Classic `:1034`), after the list hid (`:1087`, `:1026`), stored by the delegate (Mainline `:34-35`, Classic `:35-36`). Helper: Mainline `:43-45` and `SetAttribute("initmenu", frame)` `:48` (stored `:32-33`); Classic `:44-46`, `:49` (`:33-34`). `UIDropDownMenu_OnHide`: `if (id == 1) then UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU = nil` (`live :1347-1349`, `forever :1350-1352`, Classic `:1249-1251`) |
| S15 a Toggle or AddButton past `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS` (3 Mainline, 2 Classic) raises it and creates `DropDownList<n>` | confirmed | `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS = 3` (Mainline `:4`), `= 2` (Classic `:5`). `UIDropDownMenu_CreateFrames(level, index)`: `while ( level > UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS )` raises it and runs `CreateFrame("Button", "DropDownList"..UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS, nil, "UIDropDownListTemplate")`, then `newList:Hide()` (Mainline `:342-356`, Classic `:301-315`). Called through the delegate's `createframes` attribute (Mainline `:30-31`, Classic `:31-32`) by `ToggleDropDownMenu` (Mainline `:1053-1055`, Classic `:994-996`) and `UIDropDownMenu_AddButton` (Mainline `:429-431`, Classic `:388-390`). Ketho's FrameXML lists name `DropDownList1..3` on every flavor, so the kit makes three at load and reuses `DropDownList3` on Classic |
| S16 `ToggleDropDownMenu(n)` for `n > 1` shows `DropDownList<n>` whether or not the lists below it are shown | confirmed | `ToggleDropDownMenu` reads only `DropDownList<level>` (Mainline `:1058-1059`, Classic `:999-1000`) and never tests a lower list before `listFrame:Show()` (Mainline `:1049-1202`, Classic `:990-1139`); past level 1 it anchors to `button` or its parent (Mainline `:1166-1175`, Classic `:1105-1114`) without checking that it is shown. Without a frame it uses `UIDROPDOWNMENU_OPEN_MENU` (S6), which a level-1 Toggle sets after hiding list 1 (Mainline `:1087`, `:1095`; Classic `:1026`, `:1034`) and which stays set when that Toggle's init adds nothing, as the list is never shown (Mainline `:1195-1197`, Classic `:1134-1136`) and only list 1's `OnHide` clears it (Mainline `:1347-1349`, Classic `:1249-1251`). The Initialize helper hides every list for a frame that is not the open menu (S4), a list shown above a closed list 1 or a Menu API menu included, and Toggle runs it through `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize` (Mainline `:1193`, Classic `:1132`), so `Toggle(n, v, F)` with `F` not the open menu runs init with `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MENU_LEVEL` 1 and closes the open menu's list 1 first. A Toggle past level 1 with a nil `button` that does not close a list raises, as the client indexes it (`button:GetParent()` at Mainline `:1065`, Classic `:1004` when no frame is passed; at Mainline `:1168`, Classic `:1107` when it opens); the kit's message `ToggleDropDownMenu: no button; ...` names the caller's line (the client's text is a nil-index error) |
| S17 a function `checked` is called once, by `AddButton`, with the entry's Button, unprotected, and only for a checkable entry | confirmed | `local checked = info.checked; if type(checked) == "function" then checked = checked(button) end` (Mainline `:689-692`, Classic `:640-643`) sits inside `if not info.notCheckable then` (Mainline `:650`, Classic `:585`), with no `pcall`; an error there ends `AddButton`, and so the `init` that called it (S4). `button.checked = info.checked` stores the function (Mainline `:708`, Classic `:659`). Showing the list reads nothing again (Mainline `:1193-1202`, Classic `:1132-1139`); only `UIDropDownMenu_Refresh` calls it again (Mainline `:853-855`, Classic `:805`), from `UIDropDownMenu_RefreshAll` and the `SetSelected*` functions (Mainline `:910-960`, Classic `:857-907`), and a pick calls it on click (S7) |
| S18 `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize` at a level with no `DropDownList<level>` raises `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize: no DropDownList<level>` naming the caller | assumed (the error: source; the message and its line: kit choice) | After `init`, `local dropDownList = envTable["DropDownList"..level]; dropDownList.dropdown = frame` (Mainline `:93-94`, Classic `:92-93`) indexes a nil list when `level` is past `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MAXLEVELS` and `init` made no list there, so the client errors inside its own file. An `init` that adds a button at that level creates the list first (S15), and nothing errors |
| S19 `UIDropDownMenu_Initialize(frame, false)` neither stores nor calls `false`: the frame keeps its earlier `initialize` | confirmed | `if ( initFunction ) then UIDropDownMenu_SetInitializeFunction(frame, initFunction); initFunction(frame, level, frame.menuList) end` (Mainline `:83-86`, `forever` `:83`, Classic `:82-85`): a truthiness test, so `false` is skipped like `nil`. The rest of Initialize (helper close, `DropDownList<level>.dropdown`, display mode) still runs |
| S20 an entry with `info.text` and `info.colorCode` shows `colorCode .. text .. "\|r"`; a disabled one shows the plain text, or `disablecolor .. text .. "\|r"` with `disablecolor` | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenu_AddButton`: a disabled entry gets `info.colorCode = nil` and `disablecolor` replaces it (S13), then `if ( info.text ) then if ( info.colorCode ) then button:SetText(info.colorCode..info.text.."\|r") else button:SetText(info.text)` (Mainline `:484-490`, `forever` `:487`, Classic `:435-441`). The template's `ButtonText` is `$parentNormalText` (`Mainline/UIDropDownMenuTemplates.xml:105-111` read on GitHub at `b9be937`), so the kit's entry text and its `NormalText` hold the colored string, and the menu snapshot shows its color runs. `button.value` stays the plain `info.text` (Mainline `:584-590`), so a submenu's `UIDROPDOWNMENU_MENU_VALUE` has no color codes |
| S21 a `DropDownList<n>Button<i>` gets its `OnClick` and `OnEnter` once, from its template; reopening or refilling a list keeps an addon's `SetScript` on it | confirmed | `UIDropDownMenuButtonTemplate`'s `<Scripts>` set `OnClick` (`UIDropDownMenuButton_OnClick(self, button, down)`) and `OnEnter` (`UIDropDownMenuButton_OnEnter`) (`Mainline/UIDropDownMenuTemplates.xml:89-97` at `b9be937`, `Classic/UIDropDownMenuTemplates.xml:148-151` at `15c8a75`, both read on GitHub). `UIDropDownMenu.lua` calls `SetScript` only on the delegate (Mainline `:39`, Classic `:40`) and on the dropdown's own `Button` in `UIDropDownMenu_SetDropDownEnabled` (Mainline `:1528`, `:1552-1571`), never on a list Button, so `UIDropDownMenu_AddButton`, `ToggleDropDownMenu` and `UIDropDownMenu_Refresh` leave list Button scripts alone. The kit sets its scripts when it creates the Button; they act on the entry the Button shows when they run. A Menu API element differs: `InitializeFrame` sets `OnMouseDown`, `OnEnter` and `OnLeave` on every acquire (`Blizzard_Menu/Menu.lua:1128-1130`) |
| S22 a `ToggleDropDownMenu` misuse error raised through `C_Timer.After(0, ToggleDropDownMenu)` carries no file:line | assumed (kit choice; inferred from `C_Timer` being C) | `C_Timer.After` is a C API (`api/retail/UITimer.lua:4`), so in the client its callback has no Lua caller to name. The kit's timer closure lives in `luaext.lua`, which `ui/callsite.lua` skips whole (it also holds `hooksecurefunc`'s wrapper and `securecall`, stand-ins for C functions), so the error's level lands on a C frame. Skipping only the hook wrapper would name a `luaext.lua` line instead |

## Menu API

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg, read 2026-10-20. Blizzard paths are relative to
`mirror/gethe/<branch>/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/`; line numbers are `live` unless marked. The menu code is the
same on every branch for these rules, in two groups: Mainline (`live`; `forever` differs only by gamepad and
transition code, which shifts lines after `Menu.lua:1713`, `MenuTemplates.lua` and `MenuUtil.lua` by added
functions) and Classic (`classic`, `classic_era`, `classic_anniversary`: identical to each other after line endings;
older `Menu.lua`/`MenuTemplates.lua`, so lines are 12 to 24 lower; it lacks `SetCheckbox`/`IsCheckbox`/`HasResponder`,
which changes no rule below). `MenuConstants.lua` is identical on all five. The mirror does not hold
`Blizzard_UnitPopup`, the `ProxyConvertableMixin` file or `Blizzard_SharedXMLBase` (pools, frame factories).

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Menu API: M1 a Button `callback` is called `(data, menuInputData, menu)`; its return value is the response | confirmed | `Menu.lua:885-893` (`SecureCallResponder`): `descriptionProxy.responder(descriptionProxy:GetData(), menuInputData, menuProxy)`; `menuProxy = menu and menu:ToProxy() or nil` (`:886-887`), so `menu` is nil for a description with no open menu. `MenuTemplates.lua:294-306`: `CreateButton(text, callback, data)` makes the description with `data` and `SetResponder(callback)`. `Pick` uses the return as the response (`Menu.lua:933-937`). `forever`: same lines in `Menu.lua`, `MenuTemplates.lua:305-315`; Classic: `Menu.lua:873-881`, `MenuTemplates.lua:237-248` |
| Menu API: M2 Checkbox/radio `isSelected(data)`: argument and when it is called | confirmed (argument and render/Refresh timing); not re-read after every pick | Argument: `Menu.lua:694-699`: `self.isSelected(self:GetData())` and the initializers call `isSelected(data)` (`MenuTemplates.lua:312-319`; Vanilla and Cata variants `Vanilla/MenuVariants.lua:15,39`, `Cata/MenuVariants.lua:15,38`; `forever` `MenuTemplates.lua:351-358`; Classic `:262`). When: initializers run when a level opens and on every `ReinitializeAll` (`Menu.lua:1645-1665`), which only a `Refresh` response (or a response that reinitializes ancestor levels, `:2259-2262`) triggers, so after a pick only when the response is `Refresh`: not after `Open` on the same level, not after a closing response. It is also called once before the responder on a checkbox click: `OnButtonClick` (`MenuTemplates.lua:131-137`) plays `description:GetSoundKit()`, which calls `GetCheckboxSoundKit(description)` (`Menu.lua:734-739`, `MenuTemplates.lua:41-47`) → `description:IsSelected()`. `isSelected` nil → `false` (`Menu.lua:695-697`) |
| Menu API: M3 Checkbox/radio `setSelected` is called `(data, menuInputData, menu)` | confirmed | It is the responder: `MenuTemplates.lua:340` (checkbox), `:355` (radio) `SetResponder(onSelect)`, called by `SecureCallResponder` (`Menu.lua:885-893`, as M1). `forever`: `MenuTemplates.lua:379,394`; Classic: `:282,297` |
| Menu API: M4 `SetEnabled(fn)`: `fn(description)` | confirmed | `Menu.lua:830-844`: `SetEnabled` stores the value; `IsEnabled()` returns true for nil, a boolean as is, else `self.isEnabled(self)` where `self` is the description object the generator holds. Evaluated on every (re)initialize (`MenuTemplates.lua:109-113`), before a pick (`CanSelect`, `Menu.lua:706-708,910-913`: a disabled entry's `Pick` returns false and calls nothing), and polled every `MenuConstants.ElementPollFrequencySeconds` = `.2` while a function is set (`MenuConstants.lua:7`, `MenuTemplates.lua:173-182`). Classic: `Menu.lua:835` |
| Menu API: M5 `menuInputData = { context = MenuInputContext.MouseButton, buttonName }`; `MenuInputContext` values | confirmed | `Menu.lua:888-891`: `{ context = menuInputContext, buttonName = menuInputButtonName }`. A mouse click on a button, checkbox or radio calls `description:Pick(MenuInputContext.MouseButton, buttonName)` with the `OnClick` button name (`MenuTemplates.lua:131-137`; Classic `:74-80`). **`MenuInputContext = { None = 1, MouseButton = 2, MouseWheel = 3 }`** (`MenuConstants.lua:31-36`, identical on all five branches); `MouseWheel` comes from `DropdownButton.lua:369,374`. `Pick` asserts the context is non-nil (`Menu.lua:904`) |
| Menu API: M6 `menu` argument and the `MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu` return are the same object with `Close()` | assumed (same object: inferred, `ToProxy` body is not in the mirror; `Close()` and its reach: confirmed) | Same object, inferred: `CreateContextMenu` returns `OpenContextMenu(...)` = `menu and menu:ToProxy()` (`MenuUtil.lua:161-166`, `Menu.lua:2568-2573`); the responder's `menu` is `description.menu:ToProxy()` (`:886-887`, `description.menu` set at `:1088`); `MenuMixin:Init` registers the acquired menu frame as the proxy (`:960-961`, `:2106-2129`), but `ProxyConvertableMixin.ToProxy` is defined outside the mirror. `Close()`: `MenuProxyMixin:Close()` (`:1824-1826`) → `CloseMenu(self)` → `RemoveMenu` (`:1966-2010`), which first removes every deeper level (`CollapseMenusUntilLevel`, `:2030-2036`) and then the menu; shallower levels stay open. So `Close()` on the level-1 menu closes every level; on a submenu's `menu` (the argument for a submenu entry is that submenu, not level 1) it closes that level and deeper. The menu is the frame itself (`MenuTemplateBase`), so `Close` is a frame method, not added. An empty root (no elements) opens nothing and `CreateContextMenu` returns nil (`:2357-2361`). `forever` `Close` `:1859`; Classic `:1800` |
| Menu API: M7 `MenuResponse` values and what each does | confirmed values and effects; `Refresh` does not re-run the generator | **`MenuResponse = { Open = 1, Refresh = 2, Close = 3, CloseAll = 4 }`** (`MenuConstants.lua:17-23`). `MenuManagerMixin:AttributeRespond` (`Menu.lua:2219-2268`; `forever` `:2282-2331`; Classic `:2195-2243`): `nil` or `CloseAll` → `CloseMenus()` (every level, deepest first, `:1960-1964`) unless the entry has submenu elements that are active and `ShouldRespondIfSubmenu` is false (`:2236-2243`), in which case nothing closes; `Close` → `CloseMenu(menu)` = the entry's own menu and deeper, parents stay open (`:2245-2246`); `Refresh` → `ReinitializeAll` on the entry's menu plus on the child menu attached to the entry (`:2247-2254`); `Open` → no action (`:2255-2256`). Except for `nil`/`CloseAll`, every response then reinitializes all ancestor levels (`:2259-2262`), and `AddMenuResponseCallback` callbacks run last (`:2264-2267`). **`Refresh` does not re-run the generator**: `ReinitializeAll` (`:1658-1665`) re-calls each existing frame's initializers (`:1645-1656`), and the generator is called once, in `Menu.PopulateDescription` from `MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu` (`MenuUtil.lua:159`, `Menu.lua:2634-2637`); no other call to it exists in `Blizzard_Menu`. Only `isSelected`/enabled results are re-read, entries and text stay |
| Menu API: M8 default response per entry kind; a `setSelected` return counts | confirmed | `Pick` (`Menu.lua:903-947`; Classic `:891-935`): with no responder (`callback` nil) nothing is sent and the menu stays open (`:915-921,945`); otherwise `response` = the description's default response if truthy (`:922-926`), overridden by a truthy return of the responder (`:933-937`), then `SendResponseToMenu(response)` (`:944`). **Button: no default → `nil` → `CloseAll`: closes every level** (`MenuTemplates.lua:294-306` sets none; `Menu.lua:2233-2244`). **Checkbox: default `MenuResponse.Refresh`: stays open, re-reads `isSelected`** (`MenuTemplates.lua:341`; `forever` `:380`; Classic `:283`). **Radio: no default → `CloseAll`: closes every level** (`MenuTemplates.lua:345-357`; `forever` `:384-`; Classic `:287-`). A `setSelected` return counts exactly like a button callback's: any truthy return overrides the default, `false`/`nil` leaves the default. The pick toggles nothing itself; only the addon's `setSelected` changes state |
| Menu API: M9 `UnitPopup_OpenMenu(which, ...)` menu tag is `"MENU_UNIT_" .. which` | assumed (the concatenation is inferred; the tag format is documented) | `Blizzard_UnitPopup` is not in the mirror. `11_0_0_MenuImplementationGuide.lua:404`: "A UnitPopup menu tag is in the format MENU_UNIT_<UNIT_TYPE>, where UNIT_TYPE is one of the unit types (SELF, RAID, PARTY1, etc.) and is accompanied by a contextData table"; that `which` is `UNIT_TYPE` is inferred. Wiki `Patch_11.0.0/API_changes`: example `Menu.ModifyMenu("MENU_UNIT_SELF", function(ownerRegion, rootDescription, contextData)`, "In the case of unit popup menus, this contextual data includes attributes such as the unit token"; there is no `UnitPopup_OpenMenu` page (`API_UnitPopup_OpenMenu` and `Menu_Implementation_Guide` return 404). Corroboration only: WhisperMessenger `UI/ContactsList/ContextMenu.lua:151-152` hooks `"MENU_UNIT_FRIEND"` and `"MENU_UNIT_BN_FRIEND"`, `:204,209-220` opens with `which` `"FRIEND"` / `"BN_FRIEND"` and a contextData table |
| Menu API: M10 `Menu.ModifyMenu(tag, fn)`: `fn(owner, rootDescription, contextData)` | confirmed | `Menu.lua:2639-2660`: `callback(ownerRegion, description, description.contextData)` (`:2646,2654`). Hooks run from `Menu.PopulateDescription` after the generator (`:2634-2637`) through `SecureModifyMenu`, which returns when the root has no tag (`:2623-2632`); `contextData` is the 2nd argument of the root's `SetTag(tag, contextData)` (`:595-598`). **Also**: if a menu with that tag was already generated, `fn` is called at once on that last description (`:2642-2646`, `generatedDescriptions` `:2621`); the registration returns a handle (`:2659`); wiki `Patch_11.0.0/API_changes`: "Menus can be tagged with an arbitrary identifier for hooking." `forever`: `:2719-2740`; Classic: `:2610-2631` |
| Menu API: M11 the description setters `SetEnabled`, `SetTag`, `SetTooltip` return nothing (no chaining) | confirmed | `Menu.lua:830-832`: `SetEnabled` assigns `self.isEnabled` and returns nothing; `:595-598`: `SetTag` assigns `tag` and `contextData` and returns nothing; `MenuUtil.lua:287-292`: `SetTooltip` calls `elementDescription:SetOnEnter(...)` and returns nothing. `forever`: `Menu.lua:830`, `:595`, `MenuUtil.lua:299`; Classic: `Menu.lua:822`, `:595`, `MenuUtil.lua:279` |
| Menu API: M12 `MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu(nil, generator)`: the owner is `UIParent` | confirmed (nil → `GetAppropriateTopLevelParent()`); `UIParent` inferred (that function's body is not in the mirror) | `MenuUtil.lua:152-155`: `if not ownerRegion then ownerRegion = GetAppropriateTopLevelParent(); end`, before the generator runs (`:159`), so the generator receives it as `owner`. Same lines on `forever` and Classic |
| Menu API: M13 `Menu.ModifyMenu` checks only that the tag is a string. Kit choice: an argument error from `Menu.ModifyMenu` or `UnitPopup_OpenMenu` names the addon's line | confirmed (the tag assert and the late failure of a non-function `fn`); assumed (kit choice: the error position, where the client blames Blizzard's file; no source shows what `UnitPopup_OpenMenu` checks) | `Menu.lua:2640`: `Menu.ModifyMenu` only runs `assert(type(tag) == "string")`, so the client's message is `Menu.lua:2640: assertion failed!`; a non-function `fn` is not checked there: `Callback` wraps it (`:2653-2655`) and fails when a menu with the tag is generated, inside `securecallfunction` (`:2636`; wiki `API_securecallfunction`: "Errors that occur within the called function are not propagated to the caller"), so the error goes to the error handler and the menu still opens. The kit does the same (the error is recorded). Exception: when a menu with that tag was already generated, `ModifyMenu` calls the callback at once, outside `securecallfunction` (`:2642-2646`), so the client raises at registration; the kit does not model that call (the M10 gap above). It rejects a non-string tag with its own message at the caller's line (error level 3), since its own file path would mean nothing to the addon author. `Blizzard_UnitPopup` is not in the mirror. `forever` `:2725`; Classic `:2616` |
| Menu API: M14 entering an entry whose submenu is open keeps the deeper levels, also when the entry is disabled | confirmed | `Menu.lua:2286-2295` (`CheckForSubmenu`): deeper levels collapse only when the entry's description is not open (`:2289-2291`); `GenerateSubmenu` (`:2038-2041`) returns at once when it is. The enabled check (`:2293`: `frame:GetObjectType() ~= "Button" or frame:IsEnabled()`) gates only `GenerateSubmenu`, not the collapse, so a disabled entry keeps its own open submenu and closes any other. The guard asks `IsEnabled()` of Buttons only; elements that are not Buttons: M18. `forever` `:2349`, `:2073`; Classic `:2262`, `:2014` |
| Menu API: M15 `rebuild` (the kit's DropdownButton `GenerateMenu` path) re-renders only while a level of that menu is open; a closed or replaced menu does nothing, generator included | confirmed (in place while open); the closed case is the caller's | `DropdownButton.lua:195-200` (`RegisterMenu`): an open menu gets the new description and reinitializes without closing. `GenerateMenu` (`:255-264`) runs the generator even when the menu is closed, only to read selections; the kit's DropdownButton fake runs it itself on a throwaway root in that case, so it calls `rebuild` only for an open menu. Same lines on `forever` and Classic |
| Menu API: M16 a closed menu's frame is never reused, so a stale `menu:Close()` cannot close a newer menu | assumed (kit choice; the client pools menu frames, and the pool code that picks the frame is not in the mirror) | The client pools menu frames: the menu manager's factory is `CreateFrameFactory()` (`Menu.lua:1865`); `RemoveMenu` releases the frame to it (`:2005`, `self.frameFactory:Release(proxy)`) and `AcquireMenu` (`:2099`, through `AcquireMenuFrame` at `:2090-2092`, `frameFactory:Create(nil, "MenuTemplateBase", ResetMenu)`) takes one from it; the comments at `:1992-1995` ("a pool frame is never dereferenced and will always persist") and `:2124-2129` (a previous menu object may still be registered for the frame) show that frames are reused. Which frame `Create` hands back is decided by FrameXML Lua outside the mirror: `CreateFrameFactory` and `CreateFramePoolCollection` are FrameXML functions (Ketho `FrameXML.lua:1178-1180`), in `Blizzard_SharedXMLBase`, which the mirror does not hold; wiki `API_CreateFramePool` says the pools are "implemented in FrameXML/Pools.lua" and only that `Acquire` "Furnishes a new or reused widget". If it hands back the frame just released, the old handle is the new menu and its `Close()` closes it. The kit makes a fresh container for every level it opens, so an old handle only ever closes its own, already closed, level |
| Menu API: M17 opening a `UIDropDownMenu` list or a Menu API menu closes the other system's open levels | assumed (kit choice; the click-to-open case is inferred, unchecked) | No Blizzard Lua links the two: `Blizzard_Menu` never calls `CloseDropDownMenus` or names a `DropDownList`, and `Blizzard_SharedXML/Mainline/UIDropDownMenu.lua` never calls the menu manager, so a programmatic open leaves the other system's levels open. Each system closes its own levels on a mouse down outside them: `UIDropDownMenu_HandleGlobalMouseEvent` (`Blizzard_SharedXML/Mainline/UIDropDownMenu.lua:1305-1309`: `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN`, left or right button, not over a list → `CloseDropDownMenus()`) and the menu manager's `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN` callback (`Menu.lua:54-71`, `:1929-1940`). The kit keeps one stack for both (`w.menus`), which models the usual case, a click that opens one system while the other is open, where that mouse down would close the other; inferred: what calls `UIDropDownMenu_HandleGlobalMouseEvent` is not in the mirror, and the kit fires no `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN`. Known gaps: a programmatic open of one system closes the other's levels, which the client would leave open; and the shared stack can mix systems, e.g. a dropdown's level-2 initialize that opens a Menu API menu leaves a `DropDownList2` level at depth 2 under the Menu API level 1 (`framexml/fakes/dropdown.lua` `toggle`), where the client would keep `DropDownList1` open under its list 2 and show the Menu API menu on its own |
| Menu API: M18 a title or divider takes the enter: it collapses a sibling's submenu and opens its own children, also when disabled; it has no click | confirmed | `MenuTemplates.CreateTitle` and `CreateDivider` build `CreateFrame` descriptions, element type `"Frame"` (`MenuTemplates.lua:276-292`, `:528-534`; Classic `:227-234`, `:382-387`). `InitializeFrame` sets `OnMouseDown`, `OnEnter` and `OnLeave` on every element frame (`Menu.lua:1128-1130`, Classic `:1117`), which enables its mouse (I5). `CheckForSubmenu` (`:2286-2296`, Classic `:2262-2269`) then collapses deeper levels unless this element's submenu is open and opens the submenu when `frame:GetObjectType() ~= "Button" or frame:IsEnabled()`, so a Frame skips the enabled test; `CanOpenSubmenu` needs only children and no `DeactivateSubmenu` (`:413-415`). A Frame has no `OnClick`, so a click picks nothing. The kit still renders a title or divider as a Button (its `GetObjectType()` reads `"Button"`), and does not model the `OnMouseDown` that also runs `CheckForSubmenu` (`:2385-2391`); custom `CreateFrame`/`CreateTemplate` elements are not modelled |
| Menu API: M19 each render of a level sets `OnEnable`, `OnDisable` and `OnClick` on its button, checkbox and radio elements and then `OnMouseDown`, `OnEnter` and `OnLeave` on every element; a `ReinitializeAll` (a `Refresh` response, and the shallower levels after any response but `nil`/`CloseAll`, M7) sets only the first three. So an addon's `SetScript` or `HookScript` on those scripts is dropped then (F3), and the other three survive a `Refresh` | confirmed | `ButtonInitializer` (`MenuTemplates.lua:155-187`) ends with `SetScript("OnEnable", OnButtonEnable)`, `SetScript("OnDisable", OnButtonDisable)`, `SetScript("OnClick", OnButtonClick)` (`:184-186`; `forever` `:194-196`; Classic `:127-129`); `CreateButtonDescription` (`:189-193`) gives it to `CreateButton`, `CreateCheckbox` and `CreateRadio`; `ButtonFinalInitializer` then calls `button:SetEnabled` (`:109-113`; Classic `:57-61`), so an addon's `OnDisable` is gone before a refresh disables the button. `InitializeFrame` (`Menu.lua:1117-1132`; Classic `:1105-1120`) calls `CallInitializers` and then sets `OnMouseDown`, `OnEnter`, `OnLeave` (`:1128-1130`; Classic `:1116-1118`); `SetMenuDescription` runs it for every frame on each render (`:1222-1224`). `ReinitializeAll` (`:1658-1665`) runs `ReinitializeFrame` (`:1645-1656`), which calls `CallInitializers` only. The kit sets its own `OnClick` (the pick) and `OnEnter` (M14) and clears the others, whose client handlers only restyle the frame or run `CheckForSubmenu` on a mouse down (M18) |

## DropdownButton and attributes

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg, read 2026-10-20. Blizzard paths are relative to
`mirror/gethe/<branch>/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/`; line numbers are `live` unless marked. `DropdownButton.lua`
and `DropdownButton.xml` are identical on all five branches (compared after line endings); `forever` only adds
`HasOnlyRadioDescriptions` at `DropdownButton.lua:367-380`, so no line above it moves. `MenuTemplates.lua` has the
same `DropdownTextMixin`/`DropdownSelectionTextMixin`/`WowStyle1DropdownMixin` bodies on all five branches at other
lines (`forever`: +53; `classic`, `classic_era`, `classic_anniversary`: -148 through `UpdateToMenuSelections`, -167 from `OnShow` on, e.g.
`WowStyle1DropdownMixin`). `Mainline/MenuTemplates.xml` and `Classic/MenuTemplates.xml` are identical across
branches; which family a branch loads follows the Menu API grouping (Mainline: `live`, `forever`; Classic: `classic`,
`classic_era`, `classic_anniversary`), because the toc's `[Family]` token is not resolvable from the mirror.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| D1 a `DropdownButton` opens its menu on mouse down (`OnMouseDown`), not on click | confirmed | `DropdownButton.xml:3-17`: `<Button name="DropdownButton" mixin="DropdownButtonMixin" intrinsic="true">` (base type Button) with scripts `OnLoad`, `OnEnter`, `OnMouseDown`, `OnMouseWheel` and **no `OnClick`**; `DropdownButton.lua:100-106`: `OnMouseDown_Intrinsic` does `self:SetMenuOpen(not self:IsMenuOpen())`. Extra: a held Shift returns first and does nothing (`:101-103`); `OpenMenu` does nothing on a disabled dropdown (`:109-111`) and when already open (`:113-115`), `CloseMenu` has no such guard (`:133-141`). An intrinsic script cannot be replaced: an addon's `SetScript("OnMouseDown", fn)` leaves the intrinsic handler running before it (wiki `Intrinsic_frame`: "once an intrinsic frame script is bound to the frame, it cannot be rebound", default order "precall") |
| D2 a mouse down on a dropdown whose menu is already open closes the menu instead | confirmed | `DropdownButton.lua:105` (`not self:IsMenuOpen()` → `SetMenuOpen(false)`), `:143-149` (`SetMenuOpen`: true → `OpenMenu`, false → `CloseMenu`), `:133-141` (`CloseMenu`: `self.menu:Close()`, then `SignalUpdate`). `IsMenuOpen()` is `self.menu ~= nil` (`:275-277`); `self.menu` is cleared by the menu's closed callback (`:85-88`), so a pick that closes the menu makes the next mouse down open it again. A left mouse down is a global mouse event the dropdown handles itself, so the menu manager does not close the menu first (`HandlesGlobalMouseEvent`: `:174-176`) |
| D3 only the left mouse button opens or closes the menu on mouse down | confirmed | `DropdownButton.lua:83`: `self:RegisterForMouse("LeftButtonDown", "LeftButtonUp")` in `OnLoad_Intrinsic`; the handler ignores its button argument (`:100-106`). Wiki `API:Button_RegisterForMouse`: the call "Controls the OnMouseDown, OnMouseUp script events" and the default is `AnyUp, AnyDown`, so `OnMouseDown` fires for the left button only and a right or middle mouse down never reaches the toggle |
| D4 the generator is called as `generator(dropdown, rootDescription)` when the menu opens | confirmed | `DropdownButton.lua:233-236` ("function(dropdown, rootDescription)"), `:255-264` (`GenerateMenu`: `securecallfunction(PopulateDescription, self, menuGenerator, rootDescription)`), `:251-253` → `Menu.lua:2634-2635`: `securecallfunction(menuGenerator, ownerRegion, description, ...)`, `ownerRegion` = the dropdown. `OpenMenu` always regenerates first (`:117-118`: "Always regenerate the menu. This is important to avoid bugs related to stale menu state."), so the generator runs on every open. `ModifyMenu` hooks then run for a root with a tag (`Menu.lua:2623-2636`); a dropdown root has none unless the generator calls `SetTag` |
| D5 `SetupMenu(generator)` stores the generator and runs it at once on a shown dropdown; `GenerateMenu()` runs the generator on a closed menu too, and hands an open menu the new description | confirmed | `SetupMenu` (`DropdownButton.lua:237-249`) stores `menuGenerator` and, **when `self:IsShown()`, calls `GenerateMenu()` at once** (`:246-248`: "We need to generate the menu immediately if this dropdown is shown else selected options won't be reflected in the dropdown's text"); a frame from `CreateFrame` is shown, and otherwise the template's `OnShow` generates it (`MenuTemplates.lua:816-820`, `<OnShow method="OnShow"/>` in the template xml: `Mainline/MenuTemplates.xml:31`, `Classic/MenuTemplates.xml:34`; Classic `MenuTemplates.lua:649-653`, `forever` `:869-873`). That handler is the template's own script, not the intrinsic's (`DropdownButton.xml:11-16` has no `OnShow`), so a plain `DropdownButton` does not generate on show and an addon's `SetScript("OnShow", fn)` replaces it. The handler calls the frame's `OnShow` method from the template's mixin, so `hooksecurefunc(dropdown, "OnShow", fn)` runs on show too. **`GenerateMenu` is not a no-op when the menu is closed**: with a generator it builds a new root, runs the generator and `RegisterMenu`s it (`:255-264`, `:178-204`), with none it returns (`:256-259`). **On an open menu** `RegisterMenu` hands the new description to the open menu, which reinitializes without closing (`:195-201`: `self.menu:SetMenuDescription(menuDescription)`), so `SetupMenu(newGenerator)` on a shown dropdown with an open menu shows the new generator's entries in place (`:239`, `:246-248`, `:200`). So the generator runs at `SetupMenu` (shown), at every `OpenMenu`, at every `GenerateMenu`, on `OnShow`, and after each pick when `EnableRegenerateOnResponse` was called (`:290-304`) |
| D6 `SetDefaultText(text)` and `OverrideText(text)`: the override shows when both are set; the value text follows the selected radio or checkbox unless overridden | confirmed | `MenuTemplates.lua:738-741` (`SetDefaultText`: `self.defaultText = text; UpdateText()`), `:751-758` (`OverrideText`: **a nil text returns, changes nothing**; else `disableSelectionText = true; SetText(text)`), `:674-681` (`DropdownTextMixin:GetText` returns `self.text`; `SetText` stores `self.text` and runs `UpdateText`), `:687-688` (`UpdateText`: `self.Text:SetText(self:GetUpdateText())`), `:730-732` (`GetUpdateText`: `self.text or self.defaultText`). The override wins, also when `SetDefaultText` comes later. The value text does follow the selection unless overridden: `UpdateToMenuSelections` (`:760-795`) sets `text or self.defaultText` from the texts of the selected (radio or checkbox) descriptions joined with `LIST_DELIMITER`, and runs from `SignalUpdate` (`DropdownButton.lua:279-288`: after assigning a menu, a response, a menu change, `CloseMenu`). `forever` `MenuTemplates.lua:791,804,813`; Classic `:590,603,612` |
| D7 `WowStyle1DropdownTemplate` (Mainline: live, forever) | confirmed | `Mainline/MenuTemplates.xml:3-39`: `<DropdownButton name="WowStyle1DropdownTemplate" mixin="WowStyle1DropdownMixin" virtual="true">`, **no `inherits`** (the element is the intrinsic type `DropdownButton`, whose own mixin is `DropdownButtonMixin`, `DropdownButton.xml:3`); `<Size x="120" y="25"/>`; children `Background` (Texture, BACKGROUND layer, atlas `common-dropdown-textholder`), `Arrow` (Texture, OVERLAY, atlas `common-dropdown-a-button`), `Text` (FontString, OVERLAY, `inherits="GameFontHighlight"`, `wordwrap="false"`, `justifyH="LEFT"`, size 0x10); scripts `OnLoad`, `OnShow`, `OnMouseDown`, `OnMouseUp`, `OnEnter`, `OnLeave`, `OnEnable`, `OnDisable`. Mixin `WowStyle1DropdownMixin = CreateFromMixins(ButtonStateBehaviorMixin, DropdownSelectionTextMixin)` (`MenuTemplates.lua:929`; `forever` `:982`); `DropdownSelectionTextMixin` derives from `DropdownTextMixin` (`:718`). The intrinsic adds `KeyValue`s `intrinsic="DropdownButton"`, `menuPoint="TOPLEFT"`, `menuPointX=0`, `menuPointY=0`, `menuRelativePoint="BOTTOMLEFT"` (`DropdownButton.xml:4-10`) |
| D7 (Classic: classic, classic_era, classic_anniversary) same template, other values | confirmed | `Classic/MenuTemplates.xml:3-43`: same element, mixin and child keys and types (`Background`, `Arrow`, `Text`), plus `KeyValue menuMixin="MenuStyle1Mixin"` (global); `<Size x="60" y="24"/>`; atlases `common-dropdown-classic-textholder`, `common-dropdown-classic-a-buttonDown`; `Text` inherits `GameFontHighlightSmall`, `justifyH="RIGHT"`; extra script `OnSizeChanged`. Mixin at `MenuTemplates.lua:762` |
| D8 `GetObjectType()` of a `DropdownButton` is `"Button"`, its base type | assumed (wiki's general intrinsic rule; no Lua reads it) | Wiki `Intrinsic_frame`, Details: "GetObjectType returns the intrinsic's type, not the intrinsic's name e.g. `CreateFrame("MyFrame"):GetObjectType()` == "Frame", not "MyFrame"", i.e. a `DropdownButton` (declared `<Button name="DropdownButton" intrinsic="true">`, `DropdownButton.xml:3`) would report `"Button"`. Blizzard's own check supports it: `IsDropdownButtonIntrinsic(frame)` is `frame.intrinsic == "DropdownButton"` (`DropdownButton.lua:5-7`) and the intrinsic sets that key through a `KeyValue` (`DropdownButton.xml:5`), which it would not need if `GetObjectType()` told them apart. No Lua in the mirror compares `GetObjectType()` with `"DropdownButton"` |
| D9 `SetMenuOpen`, `Update`/`SignalUpdate`, `GetMenuDescription`, `HasElements`, `ClearMenuState`, `EnableRegenerateOnResponse` and `GetDefaultText` | confirmed | `DropdownButton.lua:143-149` (`SetMenuOpen`: true → `OpenMenu`, else `CloseMenu`); `:279-288`, `:330-332` (`Update` is `SignalUpdate`: re-reads the selections of `GetMenuDescription()`, no generator run, and fires the `OnUpdate` callback event); `:221-223` (`GetMenuDescription` returns `self.menuDescription`, the last generated or registered root, nil before any); `:229-231` (`HasElements`: `self.menuDescription and self.menuDescription:HasElements() or false`; `Menu.lua:409-411`: the root has any element description); `:210-215` (`ClearMenuState`: description and generator nil, then `CloseMenu`, whose `SignalUpdate` finds no selection, so the value text falls back to the default, `MenuTemplates.lua:794`); `:290-304` (`EnableRegenerateOnResponse`: a response runs `GenerateMenu` instead of `SignalUpdate`; `Menu.lua:2264-2267`: response callbacks run after the response closed or refreshed the menu); `MenuTemplates.lua:734-736` (`GetDefaultText` returns `self.defaultText`). `Menu.lua:409-411` is the same on all five branches; `Menu.lua:2267` is `forever` `:2330`, Classic `:2243`; `MenuTemplates.lua` lines are `forever` +53, Classic -148. Not modelled: the `CallbackRegistryMixin` events and `RegisterCallback` (`:69-77`), `SetSelectionText`/`SetSelectionTranslator` (`MenuTemplates.lua:743-749`: they take and return description objects, whose getters the kit's descriptions lack), `Increment`/`Decrement`/`Rotate`/`Pick`/`CollectSelectionData`/`GetSelectionData`/`HasAnyRadioDescriptions` (`:341-384`, radio traversal over `IsSelectionIgnored`/`CanSelect`, which the kit's descriptions lack; no mouse wheel input), `SetMenuAnchor`, `HandlesGlobalMouseEvent`, `HasStickyFocus`, `RegisterMenu`, `CreateRootDescription` (layout or engine hooks), and `SetTooltip`/`ShowTooltip` (`MenuTemplates.lua:830-847`) |
| D10 the value-text methods `SetText`, `GetText`, `SetDefaultText`, `GetDefaultText` and `OverrideText` belong to the template's mixin, not the intrinsic: a plain `DropdownButton` keeps `Button:SetText`/`GetText`, has no `SetDefaultText`/`GetDefaultText`/`OverrideText`, and its button text does not follow the selection | confirmed | `DropdownButtonMixin` (`DropdownButton.lua:69-392`) defines none of them, and its `UpdateToMenuSelections` is empty ("Should be implemented by the derivation if it needs to display selections", `:334-339`). They come from `DropdownTextMixin` (`MenuTemplates.lua:666-711`: `GetText`, `SetText`, `UpdateText`) and `DropdownSelectionTextMixin` (`:718-795`: `GetDefaultText`, `SetDefaultText`, `OverrideText`, `UpdateToMenuSelections`), reached through `WowStyle1DropdownMixin = CreateFromMixins(ButtonStateBehaviorMixin, DropdownSelectionTextMixin)` (`:929`), the template's `mixin` (`Mainline/MenuTemplates.xml:3`, `Classic/MenuTemplates.xml:3`). `forever` `:982`, Classic `:762` |
| D11 `WowStyle1DropdownTemplate` on a frame that is not a `DropdownButton` (`CreateFrame("Button", nil, parent, "WowStyle1DropdownTemplate")`) still creates the frame, mixin and children included; its `OnLoad` reports `<debug name> element is not a DropdownButton.` to the error handler, and the value-text methods work on it | confirmed (the report and that creation carries on); the debug-name part is the kit's `GetDebugName` (F4: an anonymous Button under `UIParent` reads `UIParent.<address>`) | `MenuTemplates.lua:931-932` (`WowStyle1DropdownMixin:OnLoad` calls `ValidateIsDropdownButtonIntrinsic(self)` first; `forever` `:984-985`, Classic `:764-765`; `<OnLoad method="OnLoad"/>` at `Mainline/MenuTemplates.xml:30`, `Classic/MenuTemplates.xml:33`); `DropdownButton.lua:1-11` (`assertsafe(IsDropdownButtonIntrinsic(frame), AssertIntrinsicMessage, frame)`, message `string.format("%s element is not a DropdownButton.", frame:GetDebugName())`, check `frame.intrinsic == "DropdownButton"`). `assertsafe` is not in the mirror: `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_SharedXMLBase/ErrorUtil.lua:8-30` read on GitHub `Gethe/wow-ui-source` at the pinned `live` commit `b9be937`: on a false condition it formats the message, passes it to `geterrorhandler()` and returns, "non-fatal" (`:10`), so nothing is raised to `CreateFrame`'s caller. `DropdownTextMixin:SetText` (`MenuTemplates.lua:678-681`) needs only `self.Text`, which the template creates. `OnShow` then calls the missing `GenerateMenu` on each show, an error in the client too (`:816-820`) |
| A1 `SetAttribute(name, value)` accepts non-string values; attribute names are case-insensitive | confirmed | Blizzard Lua passes booleans: `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/Menu.lua:2274` (`MenuAttributeDelegate:SetAttribute("respond", true)`), `:95` (`self:SetAttribute("respond", false)`), `:2271-2273` (a menu, a description and a response object); `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_SharedXML/Mainline/UIDropDownMenu.lua:431` (`UIDropDownMenuDelegate:SetAttribute("createframes", true)`), `:1054` (number 0). Wiki `API_Frame_SetAttribute`: `Frame:SetAttribute(attributeName, value)`, "value: any", attributeName "(case-insensitive)". The doc's `cstring` type (`api/retail/SimpleFrameAPI.lua:1475-1480`) is wrong for values. WhisperMessenger's call `button:SetAttribute("useOnKeyDown", true)` is the same shape |
| A2 `SetAttribute` fires `OnAttributeChanged(self, name, value)` on frames whose type lists that handler, also when the value is unchanged; `name` is lowercased | confirmed | wiki `UIHANDLER_OnAttributeChanged`: "Fires when an attribute is modified on a frame.", signature `(self, name, value)`, name "The lowercased name of the attribute that was modified.", "triggered by Frame:SetAttribute()", "will always be invoked even if the previous and newly assigned values for an attribute are identical"; `API_Frame_SetAttribute`: calling it "triggers the OnAttributeChanged script handler", `SetAttributeNoHandler` does not. Blizzard Lua registers it: `Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_Menu/Menu.lua:83` (`MenuAttributeDelegate:SetScript("OnAttributeChanged", function(self, attribute, value)`), `UIDropDownMenu.lua:29-39`. Verdict: the kit lowercases the name for storage and for the handler argument. `SetAttributeNoHandler` (the same store without the handler; test `test_set_attribute_no_handler_stores_without_the_handler` in `tests/ui/test_behaviour_attributes.lua`) is modelled; the `GetAttribute(prefix, name, suffix)` form is not |
| A3 `SetAttribute(nil, value)` and `GetAttribute(nil)` raise `SetAttribute: attributeName must be a string` (or `GetAttribute: ...`) at the caller's line | assumed (no Lua or wiki page shows what the client does with a nil name; the message text is the kit's) | wiki `API_Frame_SetAttribute` and `API_Frame_GetAttribute` type `attributeName` as "string" and do not mark it optional; the doc marks it `nilable = false` for both (`api/retail/SimpleFrameAPI.lua:437-445`, `:1467-1474`). The kit's widget argument check lets a nil through (every argument nilable), so the rule lives in `ui/behaviour_attributes.lua`; a non-string, non-nil name from addon code still gets the doc check's `bad argument #1 'attributeName'` |
| D12 the value text follows the menu's selection on a frame whose `WowStyle1DropdownMixin:OnLoad` ran: a template frame, or a plain `DropdownButton` that copied the mixin and called `OnLoad` itself | confirmed (with OnLoad); the no-OnLoad case is a kit gap | `WowStyle1DropdownMixin:OnLoad` calls `DropdownSelectionTextMixin.OnLoad(self)` (`MenuTemplates.lua:931-935`; `forever` `:984-987`, Classic `:764-767`), which sets `self.selectionTranslator` (`:724-728`; `forever` `:777-780`, Classic `:576-579`); `UpdateToMenuSelections` (`:760-795`) calls it on each selection. So what follows the selection is the mixin's methods plus that `OnLoad`, not the template name. The kit sets a flag in its mixin's `OnLoad` and keys on it. Gap: a frame that copied the mixin without running `OnLoad` errors in the client when a selection exists (`self.selectionTranslator` is nil, `:780`) and shows the default text when none does; the kit leaves its value text alone |

## Animations

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg, read 2026-10-20. No Lua in the mirror calls `CreateAnimation`.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| N1 `AnimationGroup:CreateAnimation()` with no type, or the type `"Animation"`, creates a base `Animation` | assumed (no Lua shows it) | wiki `API_AnimationGroup_CreateAnimation`: `animation = AnimationGroup:CreateAnimation([animationType, name, inheritsFrom])`, animationType "Type of the animation to create, one of: Animation (default), Translation, Rotation, Alpha, Scale, ControlPoints and Path". The doc marks `animationType` nilable (`api/retail/SimpleAnimGroupAPI.lua:8-13`). The kit matches the type ignoring case, as it does for the other animation types |

## Frames

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| F1 `SetParent` to the frame itself or one of its descendants raises `<name>:SetParent(): Would create a loop parenting to <parent>` and keeps the old parent | assumed (no Lua or wiki page shows it) | Forum report of the client error: WoWInterface thread t-45328 (2012), `kgPanel16:SetParent(): Would create a loop parenting to kgPanel16`. The wiki page `API_ScriptRegion_SetParent` lists no errors. The kit names an unnamed frame by its type and raises at the caller's line |
| F2 `CreateFrame(type, name)` sets `_G[name]` with the whole name as one key, dots included (`"My.Frame"` is not `My.Frame`) | assumed (no Lua shows the engine side) | wiki `API_CreateFrame`: `name` is the "Globally accessible name to assign to the frame, or `nil` for an anonymous frame."; the page names no path splitting |
| F3 `HookScript(script, fn)` on a script with no handler sets `fn` as the handler: `GetScript` returns it and a later `SetScript` replaces it. `SetScript`, to a function or to `nil`, drops every hook added before it. A dispatch runs the hook list the script had when it began, including hooks added to that list during the dispatch, so a script that calls `SetScript` on itself while it runs still has its earlier hooks run after it, and a hook it adds with `HookScript` runs in the same dispatch unless a `SetScript` during the dispatch replaced the list first | assumed (no Lua shows the engine side; mid-dispatch: kit choice, no source) | wiki `API_ScriptObject_HookScript` (read 2026-10-20): "If the script type doesn't have an existing handler, this will be equivalent to SetScript", its patch history: "Behaves like SetScript when the widget has no script", and "Any hooks applied will remain in place until replaced or cleared with SetScript." So `s:pick` clicks a list button whose only `OnClick` is a hook, and `SetScript(fn)`, `HookScript(h1)`, `SetScript(nil)`, `HookScript(h2)` runs only `h2`. The page says nothing about a `SetScript` from inside the running script; the kit's choice: a script that clears itself with hook `h1` runs `script, h1`, then nothing, and one that sets `script2` runs `script, h1`, then `script2`. A script with hook `h1` that calls `HookScript(h2)` runs `script, h1, h2` in that dispatch. One that calls `SetScript(script2)` and then `HookScript(h2)` runs `script, h1`, and `h2` first runs on the next dispatch, after `script2`. Known gap: `HookScript` of `OnEnter`/`OnLeave`/`OnMouseDown`/`OnMouseUp` on a script with no handler does not enable the mouse the way `SetScript` does (I5, `SetScript` in `ui/behaviour_input.lua`); no source says whether the client's does. The `bindingType` argument is not modelled. A `HookScript` whose script is not a function raises `HookScript: script must be a function` at the caller and hooks nothing: the docs mark the argument not nilable (`api/retail/SimpleScriptRegionAPI.lua:410-423`); the message is the kit's (assumed) |
| F4 `GetDebugName()` returns a named object's name; an anonymous object with a parent gets `<parent's debug name>.<key>`, `key` being the parent field that holds it, else its address; an anonymous object without a parent gets `""`. Kit choices: the address is the Lua table's, and `preferParentKey` is ignored | confirmed (name, parent key, `""`); assumed (kit choice: the address text, the field scan at call time, the parent's debug name for a deeper chain, `preferParentKey`) | wiki `API_Object_GetDebugName` examples: `named:GetDebugName() -- "SomeTexture"`, `anon:GetDebugName() -- "SomeFrame.156f1390860"` for an anonymous texture of `SomeFrame`, `a:GetDebugName() == "" -- true` for an unparented anonymous frame, and `CharacterFrame.b:GetDebugName() -- "CharacterFrame.b"` once `b`, stored as `CharacterFrame.b`, is parented to it; `CharacterFrame.TitleText:GetDebugName() -- "CharacterFrameTitleText"` (a global name wins over the key). The page gives `preferParentKey` (`boolean?`, default `false`) no example, and no example of an anonymous parent; the doc gives the argument and a non-nilable string return (`api/retail/SimpleObjectAPI.lua:11-29`) |
| F5 a FontString with no text set (fresh, or after `SetText(nil)`) returns nil from `GetText()` | assumed (wiki `API:FontString_GetText`) | wiki `API:FontString_GetText`: `text : string?` - "The text of the FontString; or nil if no text is set." Ketho's doc disagrees: it marks the return `nilable = false`, type `cstring`, on every flavor (`api/retail/SimpleFontStringAPI.lua:409-416`; `forever` `:409`; `mists`, `tbc`, `vanilla` `:401`); the kit follows the wiki. EditBox `GetText` returns `""` instead (I12) |
| F6 the lazy names ChatFrame1EditBox-ChatFrame10EditBox are EditBoxes (template and parent not modelled) | confirmed (extract) retail; assumed on other flavors | Blizzard FrameXML, local extract retail build 70575, code/addons/blizzard_chatframebase/mainline/floatingchatframe.xml:540 |
| F7 Mixin and CreateFromMixins raise on a mixin that is not a table, at the caller's line; message in the kit's argument format | assumed (nilability confirmed) | Blizzard FrameXML, local extract retail build 70575, code/addons/blizzard_apidocumentationgenerated/framescriptdocumentation.lua:322-336 (mixins Nilable = false) |
| F8 the lazy names ChatFrame1-ChatFrame10 are ScrollingMessageFrames (an intrinsic over Frame, so `GetObjectType` answers `Frame`; template, mixin and parent not modelled) | confirmed (extract) retail and tbc; assumed on other flavors | Blizzard FrameXML, local extract retail build 70575, code/addons/blizzard_chatframebase/mainline/floatingchatframe.xml:698-751; tbc build 70577, code/addons/blizzard_chatframebase/classic/floatingchatframe.xml:716-830; test `test_chat_frame_names_resolve_to_scrolling_message_frames` in `tests/framexml/test_frames.lua` |

## Addon loading

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| L1 a file whose `[AllowLoadGameType ...]` names only `wrath`, `cata`, `plunderstorm` and/or `wowhack` loads on none of the kit's flavors; a list naming no game type the client knows loads everywhere | assumed (which types each client build recognizes is not documented) | wiki `TOC_format`, `AllowLoadGameType`: game types `standard`, `forever`, `camelot`, `mists`, `cata`, `wrath`, `tbc`, `vanilla`, `plunderstorm`, `wowhack`; families `mainline` and `classic` ("classic" covers `vanilla`, `tbc`, `wrath`, `cata`, `mists`); "If at least 1 game type is specified and none of the game types are recognized by the client, the condition will be satisfied." The kit counts `wrath`, `cata`, `plunderstorm` and `wowhack` as known on every flavor. The kit's forever flavor answers to `forever`, `camelot` and `mainline` (L6) |
| L2 `setmetatable(_G, mt)` in addon code gives the globals table `mt`: its `__index` runs only for absent globals, its `__newindex` only for new ones, `getmetatable(_G)` returns it (nil before any call; its `__metatable` field when set, and then `setmetatable(_G, ...)` raises "cannot change a protected metatable"), handlers get the addon's `_G` as their table, a handler's `error(msg, 2)` names the addon line that read or wrote the global, and every addon of the world sees the same one. Kit choice: the kit keeps `mt` beside its sandbox view and never sets it on the real `_G`, so kit and Blizzard-fake code reading `_G` does not see it | assumed (no Lua or wiki page shows the client's `_G` metatable) | Lua 5.1 reference manual §2.8: the "index" and "newindex" events run only when the key is absent (`settable_event`: `local v = rawget(table, key)` / `if v ~= nil then rawset(table, key, value); return end`, and the same `rawget` check in `gettable_event`), and "You can replace the metatable of tables through the `setmetatable` function."; the argument and `__metatable` checks are `luaB_setmetatable`/`luaB_getmetatable` in Lua 5.1 `lbaselib.c` (the kit's tests compare its messages to the host Lua 5.1's); the client has one `_G` for every addon. Whether Blizzard code reading a missing global would hit an addon's `__index` in the client is unsettled. Known gap: the kit's `setmetatable`/`getmetatable` are Lua functions, so a misuse in tail position (`return setmetatable({}, 5)`) raises without the addon's file:line, where the client's C function keeps it. Known gap: a handler's `error(msg, 3)` or any higher level loses the addon's location (kit `lvl3 L3`, plain Lua 5.1 `addon:H1/Core.lua:23: lvl3 L3`): the kit runs the handler under `pcall`, so level 3 lands on that C frame and the message carries no position the kit could replace. Known gap: the misuse messages always name `setmetatable`/`getmetatable` and count arguments from the first; Lua 5.1's `luaL_argerror` (`lauxlib.c`) names the function the way the caller reached it (`'smt'` after `local smt = setmetatable`, `'?'` for `pcall(setmetatable, {}, 5)`) and does not count `self` in a method call (`o:setmetatable(5)` is `bad argument #1`). Known gap: a handler that reads its own global again (`__index = function(_, k) return _G[k] end`) overflows with `C stack overflow` naming either the handler's line (`addon:<Addon>/Core.lua:1: C stack overflow`) or no line at all, depending on how many C calls are already on the stack (one more flips it; `tests/test_addon_globals_overflow.lua`), where plain Lua 5.1 always names the handler's line: each kit cycle makes two C calls, the addon's read and the kit's `pcall` around the handler, and the limit can land on the `pcall`, which has no line |
| L3 `getfenv()`, `getfenv(1)` and `getfenv(f)` for any addon's function return `_G`, the same table addon code reads as `_G`. Kit choice: that table is the kit's view of `_G`, which hides the host-only names, not the host's real `_G` | confirmed (one shared `_G`); assumed (kit choice: the view stands in for `_G`) | wiki `API:getfenv`: "Returns the current environment in use by the function.", `f` "a numeric level of a function on the call stack starting from 1"; Lua 5.1 reference manual `getfenv`: "Level 1 is the function calling getfenv", default 1; wiki `Using_the_AddOn_namespace`: the namespace lets files share data "without exposing or polluting variables to the global environment", so every addon's files run in the one global environment. The kit runs each addon in its own environment table in front of the view, and `getfenv` answers the view for it and for every other addon's. Misuse errors name `getfenv` at the addon's line, as Lua 5.1's do (`getfenv(100)`: `bad argument #1 to 'getfenv' (invalid level)`). Known gap: a level-1 `getfenv` in tail position (`return getfenv()`) raises `'getfenv' cannot reach the calling function: a tail call (return getfenv(...)) replaced it`, where Lua 5.1 returns the function's environment: the kit's `getfenv` is a Lua function, so the tail call replaces the addon's function on the stack (`tests/test_addon_fenv_errors.lua`). Known gap: the kit reaches addon code through a tail call (L4), so `getfenv(2)` from a file's main chunk raises `no function environment for tail call at level 2` (`pcall(getfenv, 3)`: `... at level 3`), where Lua 5.1 returns `_G`: the main chunk's caller is C (inferred: the client's loader returns `_G` too, as a C caller; no source shows its stack). Known gap: under `pcall` (`pcall(getfenv, 100)`) the misuse message names `getfenv`, where Lua 5.1 names `'?'`. Known gap: as a coroutine body, `coroutine.wrap(getfenv)` and `coroutine.create(getfenv)` are accepted and the call raises `bad argument #1 to 'getfenv' (invalid level)`, where Lua 5.1 rejects them at the caller's line with `bad argument #1 to 'wrap'` (or `'create'`) `(Lua function expected)` (`'?'` under `pcall`): the kit's `getfenv` is a Lua function |
