## Game state

Wiki pages read 2026-10-27 (`warcraft.wiki.gg`, fetched through a summarising reader, so a quoted sentence is secondhand; no page 404ed, but `API_IsShiftKeyDown` and `API_IsLeftShiftKeyDown` returned the `IsModifierKeyDown` page, `API_UnitBuff` and `API_UnitDebuff` the `C_UnitAuras` pages, `API_UnitFullName` and `API_GetUnitName` the `UnitName` page, `API_GetRealmName` the `GetNormalizedRealmName` page and `API_ReloadUI` the `C_UI.Reload` page); signatures from `api/<flavor>/` (a `file:line` without a flavor is `api/retail/`; the other four flavors hold the same key unless the row says so; names in `globals.api` alone have no signature in the data); Blizzard citations are `path:line` in the local client extract (retail build 70575, `code/addons/<path>`, never committed); the repo was at `7cc6549`. The Phase 7 plan's bracketed expectations were checked, not copied: where a source disagrees the row states what the source says.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| G1 a name is installed only on the flavors whose `api/<flavor>` has it (`functions`, `globals.api` or `globals.framexml`). On all five flavors: `GetCVar`, `GetCVarBool`, `SetCVar`, `GetUnitName`, `ReloadUI` (`globals.framexml`). On mists, tbc and vanilla only: `GetSpellInfo`, `GetSpellTexture`, `GetSpellLink`, `GetSpellCooldown`. Also installed, as Blizzard's deprecation fallbacks define them (`loadDeprecationFallbacks` defaults to 1 in release builds): bare `SendChatMessage` and `IsSpellKnown` on all five flavors; `GetSpecialization` and `GetSpecializationInfo` on retail and forever; `UnitAura`, `UnitBuff` and `UnitDebuff` on mists, tbc and vanilla. Each fallback forwards to its replacement as the fallback file does. On no flavor: `GetItemInfo`, `GetItemInfoInstant`, `GetItemCount`, `GetItemIcon`, `GetAddOnMetadata`, `GetAddOnInfo`, `GetNumAddOns`, `IsAddOnLoaded`, `LoadAddOn`, `EnableAddOn`, `DisableAddOn`, `RunMacroText` (the plan expected the removed globals on vanilla, tbc and mists; the data, the wiki and the extract's fallbacks say none, and the data holds the `C_Spell`, `C_Item`, `C_AddOns`, `C_UnitAuras`, `C_SpecializationInfo`, `C_SpellBook`, `C_ChatInfo`, `C_Macro` and `C_CVar` replacements on all five flavors) | confirmed (presence per flavor in the data; the wiki availability lists agree; the fallback files and their game types in retail build 70575: `blizzard_deprecated.toc` loads `Classic/*` with `[AllowLoadGameType classic]`, `blizzard_deprecatedspecialization.toc` has `## AllowLoadGameType: classic, standard` and only `deprecated_specialization_standard.lua` defines the two names, `blizzard_deprecatedchatinfo.toc` and `blizzard_deprecatedspellbook.toc` have no game-type limit; wiki `CVar_loadDeprecationFallbacks`: "1 (default, see notes)", "In release builds it is generally enabled, however it can default to disabled in test builds."); assumed (that classic clients ship the same classic fallbacks as this retail build's files; that forever loads the standard files; a kit CVar to turn the fallbacks off is not modelled) | `api/<flavor>/globals.lua` and the `functions` of `api/<flavor>/*.lua`, searched for each name on all five flavors: `GetSpell*` is in `globals.api` of mists, tbc and vanilla only; the 19 listed names are in no file; `GetCVar`, `GetCVarBool`, `SetCVar`, `GetUnitName`, `ReloadUI` are in `globals.framexml` of all five. Wiki availability (the reader reads `+` as added and `-` as removed): `GetItemInfo`, `GetItemInfoInstant`, `GetItemCount`, `GetItemIcon` end at 10.2.6 (standard), 4.4.0 (mists), 2.5.5 (tbc), 1.15.2 (vanilla); `IsAddOnLoaded`, `GetNumAddOns`, `LoadAddOn`, `EnableAddOn`, `DisableAddOn`, `GetAddOnInfo` at 10.2.0, 4.4.0, 2.5.5, 1.15.0; `GetAddOnMetadata` at 10.1.0, 3.4.2, 2.5.5, 1.14.4; `RunMacroText` at 11.0.0, 4.4.1, 2.5.5, 1.15.4; `GetSpellInfo`, `GetSpellTexture`, `GetSpellLink`, `GetSpellCooldown` end at 11.0.0 on standard and have no end marker on mists, tbc, vanilla; `GetSpecialization`, `GetSpecializationInfo` and `IsSpellKnown` are "deprecated in patch 11.2.0 ... and will be removed in the future" (`SendChatMessage`: "deprecated in patch 11.2.0"); `UnitBuff` and `UnitDebuff` redirect to `C_UnitAuras.GetBuffDataByIndex` and `GetDebuffDataByIndex`; `UnitAura` is "Removed, replaced by C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDataByIndex" in 11.0.2. Extract: `blizzard_deprecated/classic/deprecated_1_15_8.lua:10-33` and `deprecated_2_5_5.lua` define `UnitAura`, `UnitBuff`, `UnitDebuff`; `blizzard_deprecatedspecialization/deprecated_specialization_standard.lua:13,16` defines `GetSpecializationInfo` and `GetSpecialization`; `blizzard_deprecatedspellbook/deprecated_spellbook.lua:16` `IsSpellKnown`; `blizzard_deprecatedchatinfo/deprecated_chatinfo.lua:8` `SendChatMessage`; each file starts `if not GetCVarBool("loadDeprecationFallbacks") then return; end`. No extract file defines the bare `GetItemInfo`, `GetAddOnMetadata`, `IsAddOnLoaded` or `RunMacroText` |
| G2 `GetLocale` returns the client locale code (`enUS`, `koKR`, `frFR`, `deDE`, `zhCN`, `esES`, `zhTW`, `esMX`, `ruRU`, `ptBR`, `itIT`; an `enGB` client reports `enUS`); `GetRealmName` the display name; `GetNormalizedRealmName` the name without spaces, hyphens or periods (the plan said spaces and hyphens), and it may be nil before `PLAYER_LOGIN` and between `LOADING_SCREEN_ENABLED` and `LOADING_SCREEN_DISABLED` | confirmed | `Locale.lua:63` `GetLocale() -> localeName`; `Connection.lua:96` `GetRealmName() -> realmName`; `PlayerScript.lua:823` `GetNormalizedRealmName() -> result`; wiki `API_GetLocale`: "Returns the game client locale."; wiki `API_GetNormalizedRealmName`: `normalizedRealm`, "string? - The name of the realm without spaces, `-` hyphens, or `.` periods.", "When logging in, `GetNormalizedRealmName()` only returns information starting from as early as the" `PLAYER_LOGIN` event, and it "may return nil between the" two loading-screen events |
| G3 `GetCVar(name)` returns the value as a string and nil for an invalid name; `GetCVarBool` is true for "1" and false for "0"; `SetCVar(name, value)` stores the string form (a boolean stores "1" or "0", a number its `tostring`); whether names are case-insensitive is not stated | confirmed (the string return, nil for an invalid name, "1" and "0", the conversion in `SetCVar`); assumed (case-insensitive names; `GetCVarBool` of any other value or of an unknown name, the wiki types it `boolean?`) | `blizzard_sharedxmlbase/cvarutil.lua:9-15` (`SetCVar`: a boolean sends `"1"` or `"0"`, else `value and tostring(value) or nil`), `:17` (`GetCVar` returns `C_CVar.GetCVar(name)`), `:33` (`GetCVarBool`); wiki `API_GetCVar`: "value string? - current value of the CVar", an invalid name "will return nil", and no word on case; wiki `API_GetCVarBool`: "Compared to GetCVar, "1" would return as true, "0" would return as false"; wiki `API_SetCVar`: `value` is a string or number, nothing on conversion |
| G4 `IsLoggedIn()` is false (the wiki says nil) before `PLAYER_LOGIN` fires and true from the event on | confirmed (not logged in before the event); assumed (that it is already true inside `PLAYER_LOGIN` handlers) | wiki `API_IsLoggedIn`: "Returns nil before the PLAYER_LOGIN event has fired." `blizzard_sharedxml/eventutil.lua:77-84` (`EventUtil.ContinueOnPlayerLogin` runs the callback at once when `IsLoggedIn()` is true, else waits for `PLAYER_LOGIN`); `PlayerScript.lua:1828` `IsLoggedIn() -> result`. Nothing orders the flag against handler dispatch |
| G5 `GetServerTime()` is Unix seconds; `GetFramerate()` is the frame rate; `GetNetStats()` returns four numbers, `bandwidthIn`, `bandwidthOut` (KB/s), `latencyHome`, `latencyWorld` (ms), where the kit's data lists three (`in, out, latencyList`); the kit values 60 and 0.5, 0.5, 30, 40 are kit choices | confirmed (`GetServerTime` is seconds since the epoch and Blizzard does arithmetic on it; the four `GetNetStats` returns); assumed (whole seconds that advance with the kit clock; the values 60 and 0.5, 0.5, 30, 40) | `SystemTime.lua:42` `GetServerTime() -> time`; wiki `API_GetServerTime`: "number - Time in seconds since the epoch", examples are integers; `blizzard_communities/communitiesticketmanagerdialog.lua:62` (`expirationTime / 1000000 - GetServerTime()`); `Client.lua:54` `GetFramerate() -> framerate`, wiki "Returns the current framerate."; `Connection.lua:45` `GetNetStats() -> in, out, latencyList` (all five flavors) against `blizzard_performancebar/performancebar.lua:60` (`bandwidthIn, bandwidthOut, latencyHome, latencyWorld = GetNetStats()`) and wiki `API_GetNetStats` (four returns; latency "only updated every 30 seconds"). The wiki and Blizzard's call sites win over the data |
| G6 `IsModifierKeyDown` is true while any modifier key is down (Control, Shift, Alt and Meta, each left or right; Meta is Command on a Mac); `IsShiftKeyDown`, `IsControlKeyDown`, `IsAltKeyDown` are true for either side; `IsLeftShiftKeyDown` and its siblings test one side | confirmed (`IsModifierKeyDown` and its signature); assumed (either side for the three generic functions and one side for the `IsLeft*` and `IsRight*` ones: the reader found no sentence, only the page's call hierarchy placing the two sided functions under each generic one) | wiki `API_IsModifierKeyDown`: "Returns true if a modifier key is currently pressed down.", "So-called Meta key is the Command key in Mac clients."; `Input.lua:112` `IsAltKeyDown`, `:125` `IsControlKeyDown`, `:206` `IsLeftShiftKeyDown`, `:232` `IsModifierKeyDown`, `:319` `IsShiftKeyDown`, each `-> down`; mists, tbc and vanilla list them in `globals.api` only |
| G7 `IsModifiedClick(action)` is true when the action's configured modifier is held. Defaults: SELFCAST ALT, FOCUSCAST ALT (the plan said NONE), MOUSEOVERCAST NONE, PICKUPACTION SHIFT, AUTOLOOTTOGGLE SHIFT; CHATLINK SHIFT, DRESSUP CTRL and SPLITSTACK SHIFT are kit values. `IsMouseButtonDown(button?)` with no argument is true for any button; `GetMouseButtonClicked()` is the button of the innermost `OnClick` being handled | confirmed (the action names; the five Settings-panel defaults; `IsMouseButtonDown`; `GetMouseButtonClicked` during `OnClick`); assumed (CHATLINK SHIFT, DRESSUP CTRL, SPLITSTACK SHIFT, which no Lua sets; that the Settings default is the engine's initial value; `IsModifiedClick()` with no argument as "any modified click"; `GetMouseButtonClicked` outside a click) | wiki `API_IsModifiedClick`: action names AUTOLOOTTOGGLE, CHATLINK, COMPAREITEMS, DRESSUP, FOCUSCAST, OPENALLBAGS, PICKUPACTION, QUESTWATCHTOGGLE, SELFCAST, SHOWITEMFLYOUT, SOCKETITEM, SPLITSTACK, STICKYCAMERA, TOKENWATCHTOGGLE, defaults not given. Defaults passed to `Settings.RegisterModifiedClickSetting` (`blizzard_settings_shared/blizzard_settings.lua:187`, which reads and writes `GetModifiedClick` and `SetModifiedClick` at `blizzard_settings_shared/blizzard_setting.lua:364-374`): `blizzard_settingsdefinitions_frame/combat.lua:127` SELFCAST `"ALT"`, `:145` FOCUSCAST `"ALT"`, `:57` MOUSEOVERCAST `"NONE"`; `actionbars.lua:100` PICKUPACTION `"SHIFT"`; `controls.lua:65` AUTOLOOTTOGGLE `"SHIFT"`. A bare `IsModifiedClick()` is called at `blizzard_achievementui/mainline/blizzard_achievementui.lua:1103` and `blizzard_actionbar/shared/petactionbar.lua:264`, which fits any meaning. ## Combat

Wiki pages read 2026-10-27 (`warcraft.wiki.gg`, fetched through a summarising reader, so a quoted sentence is secondhand; `CVar_nameplateMaxDistance` and `CVar_nameplateShowEnemies` returned 404, so those two rows come from the `Console_variables` page, read only from character 250000 to 350000 of 466778); signatures from `api/<flavor>/` (a `file:line` without a flavor is `api/retail/`; the other four flavors hold the same key unless the row says so); Blizzard citations are `path:line` in the local client extract (retail build 70575, `code/addons/<path>`, never committed). The classic_era mirror (`mirror/gethe/classic_era/`, commit `8165d4c`) holds only the API documentation, `Blizzard_Menu` and `Blizzard_SharedXML`: no `Blizzard_CombatLog` and no `Blizzard_NamePlates`, so no classic_era Blizzard code was read and the per-flavor rows C3 and C11 stay `assumed` until the user runs `! bash tools/fetch.sh classic_era` (the `REFERENCE_PATHS` edit in `tools/fetch.sh` now covers both folders). The Phase 8 plan's bracketed expectations were checked, not copied: where a source disagrees the row states what the source says. Retail's Blizzard combat log code is the 12.x rewrite (`blizzard_combatlogprocessor/blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua`, fed by `C_CombatLogSecure.GetCurrentEventInfo`), and the wiki describes 12.0.0 rules, so each row says which flavors its evidence covers.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| C1 The `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED` payload starts with eleven base fields in the order `timestamp, subevent, hideCaster, sourceGUID, sourceName, sourceFlags, sourceRaidFlags, destGUID, destName, destFlags, destRaidFlags`. On retail since 12.0.0 an addon cannot receive the event: registering it errors with `ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN` (the plan expected retail to deliver it) | confirmed (the field names and order; that retail 12.0.0 refuses addon registration); assumed (that vanilla, mists, tbc and forever still deliver the event to addons: the wiki gives no flavor split, and `api/` lists the event with no payload on all five flavors) | `blizzard_combatlogprocessor/blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua:196` `GenerateMessage(filterSettings, timestamp, event, hideCaster, sourceGUID, sourceName, sourceFlags, sourceRaidFlags, destGUID, destName, destFlags, destRaidFlags, ...)`; wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT`: the page lists "timestamp", "subevent", "hideCaster", "sourceGUID", "sourceName", "sourceFlags", "sourceRaidFlags", "destGUID", "destName", "destFlags", "destRaidFlags"; wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED`: "This event is no longer accessible to addons in Midnight since Patch 12.0.0", "Fires ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN when attempting to register this event."; wiki `Patch_12.0.0/API_changes`: "COMBAT_LOG_EVENT and COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED will error when trying to register them."; `CombatLog.lua:4-6` `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED = {}` |
| C2 Prefixes: `SWING_` adds no field; `SPELL_`, `SPELL_PERIODIC_` and `RANGE_` add `spellId, spellName, spellSchool`; `ENVIRONMENTAL_` adds `environmentalType`; `DAMAGE_SHIELD` and `DAMAGE_SPLIT` also carry the three spell fields | confirmed (all four prefixes on retail's parser; the wiki is ambiguous about `RANGE_` but Blizzard's parser reads the spell fields for it); assumed (the same prefixes on the four non-retail flavors: no Blizzard code for them was read) | `blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua:291` (`SWING_DAMAGE` reads `amount` first), `:330` (`subVal == "SPELL"`, which also covers `SPELL_PERIODIC_`, reads `spellId, spellName, spellSchool`), `:747` (`RANGE_`), `:793` and `:894` (damage shield and `DAMAGE_SPLIT`), `:869` (`environmentalType, amount, ...`); wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT`: SWING_ "no prefix parameters", ENVIRONMENTAL_ "environmentalType" |
| C3 Suffix fields after the prefix, retail parser. `_DAMAGE`: `amount, overkill, school, resisted, blocked, absorbed, critical, glancing, crushing, isOffHand` (`ENVIRONMENTAL_DAMAGE` has the same list without `isOffHand`); `_MISSED`: `missType, isOffHand, amountMissed, critical`; `_HEAL`: `amount, overhealing, absorbed, critical`; `_AURA_APPLIED`, `_AURA_REMOVED`, `_AURA_REFRESH`: `auraType` then a second value that Blizzard names `remainingPoints` (absorb points left; the wiki names it `amount`); `_AURA_APPLIED_DOSE` and `_AURA_REMOVED_DOSE`: `auraType, amount`; `_CAST_FAILED`: one field, `failedType` on the wiki and `missType` in Blizzard's parser; `_INTERRUPT`: `extraSpellId, extraSpellName, extraSpellSchool`; `_DISPEL`: those three then `auraType`; `UNIT_DIED` carries `recapID, unconsciousOnDeath` (the plan said no extra fields); `_RESURRECT`: no fields; `SPELL_ABSORBED` is not parsed by retail's Blizzard code, and the wiki lists, after the optional attack spell fields, `casterGUID, casterName, casterFlags, casterRaidFlags, absorbSpellId, absorbSpellName, absorbSpellSchool, amount, critical` (the plan called the first four `absorber*`; the positions are the same) | confirmed (retail, from Blizzard's parser, for every suffix named here except `SPELL_ABSORBED`, which rests on the wiki alone); assumed (every non-retail flavor: classic_era's Blizzard code was not available and the wiki names no field added in one flavor only, so the lists are taken as shared; a mirror fetch by the user would confirm them) | `blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua:334` (`SPELL_DAMAGE`), `:348` (`SPELL_MISSED`), `:373` (`SPELL_HEAL`), `:544` (`SPELL_CAST_FAILED`: `missType = select(4, ...)`), `:586` (`SPELL_INTERRUPT`), `:645` (`SPELL_DISPEL`: `extraSpellId, extraSpellName, extraSpellSchool, auraType`), `:691` (`auraType, remainingPoints`), `:713` (`auraType, amount`), `:849` (`recapID, unconsciousOnDeath = ...`), `:869` (`ENVIRONMENTAL_DAMAGE`, no `isOffHand`); wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT`: "_AURA_APPLIED: auraType, amount", "_CAST_FAILED: failedType", "UNIT_DIED: recapID, with unconsciousOnDeath noted under Death Events", "SPELL_ABSORBED: ... casterGUID, casterName, casterFlags, casterRaidFlags, absorbSpellId, absorbSpellName, absorbSpellSchool, amount, critical" |
| C4 Empty values. A damage event always carries a number in `overkill` (Blizzard compares `overkill > 0` with no nil guard); a nil `sourceName` or `destName` disables that side; the wiki's `UNIT_DIED` example has `sourceGUID` `"0000000000000000"`, `sourceName` nil and `sourceFlags` `0x80000000` (`COMBATLOG_OBJECT_NONE`), not 0 as the plan expected | confirmed (`overkill` is a number, a nil name disables its side, the `UNIT_DIED` example values); assumed (nil for unset `resisted`, `blocked`, `absorbed`, `critical`; `destGUID` `""` and `destFlags` 0 for a no-destination event; `isOffHand` false; the value of `overkill` when nothing overkilled) | `blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua:300`, `:343`, `:440` (`if ( overkill > 0 )` with no guard), `:273` (`if ( not sourceName or C_CombatLog.DoesObjectMatchFilter(sourceFlags, Enum.CombatLogObject.None) )` disables the source), `blizzard_combatlogbase/combatlogutil.lua:205` (`if overkill and overkill > 0`); wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT`: the `UNIT_DIED` example shows the source GUID as "0000000000000000" and the source name as "nil", with flags of 0x80000000 |
| C5 `COMBATLOG_OBJECT_*` bit values: affiliation mine 0x1, party 0x2, raid 0x4, outsider 0x8 (mask 0xF); reaction friendly 0x10, neutral 0x20, hostile 0x40 (mask 0xF0); control player 0x100, npc 0x200 (mask 0x300); type player 0x400, npc 0x800, pet 0x1000, guardian 0x2000, object 0x4000 (mask 0xFC00); special target 0x10000, focus 0x20000, maintank 0x40000, mainassist 0x80000, none 0x80000000, empty 0; raid markers in `raidFlags`: `Raidtarget1` to `Raidtarget8` are 0x1 to 0x80 and `RaidNone` is 0x80000000. The player's own flags are 0x511 (mine, friendly, control player, type player); a hostile NPC is 0xA48 (outsider, hostile, control npc, type npc); with the current target bit that is 0x10A48 | confirmed (every value, from the data; the player and hostile-NPC sums follow from them and match the two flag values the wiki quotes); assumed (which bits the client sets for a party or raid member, a pet or a neutral unit beyond those sums: no source states them) | `CombatLogConstants.lua:8-14` (masks), `:21-46` (`Enum.CombatLogObject`), `:48-58` (`CombatLogObjectTarget`); `blizzard_deprecatedcombatlog/deprecated_combatlog.lua:39-62` (the `COMBATLOG_OBJECT_*` names map to those enum values); wiki `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT`: "68168 (0x10A48)" means the unit is the current target, is an NPC, and is hostile; log lines show 0x511 |
| C6 GUID shapes. Creature and Vehicle: `Creature-0-<server>-<instance>-<zone>-<npcId>-<10 hex>`, example `Creature-0-1465-0-2105-448-000043F59F`; Player: `Player-<server>-<player id>`; a cast GUID (the `castID` of `UnitCastingInfo`): `Cast-3-<server>-<instance>-<zone>-<spellId>-<10 hex>`, example `Cast-3-3890-1159-21205-8936-00014B7E7F`; on `forever` (1.60.1) the server and spawn parts of creature, vehicle, pet and object GUIDs are obfuscated values and the shape is otherwise unchanged | confirmed (the Creature and Cast shapes and examples); assumed (the Player id being 8 hex digits; the `Pet` and `GameObject` shapes beyond "same as Creature"; the meaning of the cast GUID fields after `Cast-3`) | wiki `API_UnitGUID`: example "Creature-0-1465-0-2105-448-000043F59F", the sample code splits it as `_, _, server_id, instance_id, zone_uid, npc_id, spawn_uid`, and the Player GUID as `_, server_id, player_id`; patch 1.60.1: "The server ID and spawn ID portions of creature, vehicle, pet and game object GUIDs have been replaced"; wiki `API_UnitCastingInfo`: castID "for example `Cast-3-3890-1159-21205-8936-00014B7E7F`" |
| C7 A unit event is fired once per token that names the unit, in `TOKEN_ORDER`; `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED` fires before `UNIT_TARGET`; no event fires when a value is unchanged; the combat log event comes before the unit events of the same action | assumed | no source: `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED` and `UNIT_TARGET` appear in `Unit.lua:455` and `Unit.lua:1674` with no ordering text, `blizzard_nameplates.lua:46-62` handles `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED` independently, and the wiki pages read (`UNIT_SPELLCAST_START`, `UNIT_SPELLCAST_INTERRUPTED`, `GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE`, `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED`) state no cross-event order |

Order for a cast-time spell (START, then SUCCEEDED, then STOP) and the position of INTERRUPTED relative to STOP are not stated | confirmed (the payloads, from the data and the wiki's `INTERRUPTED` signature); assumed (every ordering claim: the two wiki pages say nothing about order) | `Unit.lua:1564` (`UNIT_SPELLCAST_START`, `secretWhen = { "UnitSpellCastRestricted" }` at `:1592`), `:1594` (`_STOP`), `:1624` (`_SUCCEEDED`), `:1433` (`_INTERRUPTED`: `unitTarget, castGUID, spellID, interruptedBy, castBarID`), `:1175` (`_CHANNEL_STOP`); wiki `UNIT_SPELLCAST_INTERRUPTED`: "UNIT_SPELLCAST_INTERRUPTED: unitTarget, castGUID, spellID, interruptedBy, castBarID" |

On retail the returns are secret under `UnitThreatStateRestricted` and `UnitThreatValuesRestricted` | confirmed (the signatures, return names and the 0 to 3 meanings; the nil return); assumed (what the payload unit of each event is relative to the threat list: the data gives only the name `unitTarget`) | `Unit.lua:7042` (`UnitThreatSituation`, `mobGUID` nilable, `result` nilable), `Unit.lua:3622` (`UnitDetailedThreatSituation`: `isTanking, status, scaledPercentage, rawPercentage, rawThreat`), `Unit.lua:1694` and `:1704` (the two events); the same names on vanilla, mists, tbc and forever; wiki `API_UnitThreatSituation`: "A `nil` return means the unit is not on the mob's threat table."; wiki `API_UnitDetailedThreatSituation`: "isTanking, status, scaledPercentage, rawPercentage, rawThreat = UnitDetailedThreatSituation(unit, mobGUID)" |

| C16 (retail) Secret values. `issecretvalue(value)` returns true for a secret value; `canaccesssecrets`, `hasanysecretvalues`, `scrubsecretvalues` (secrets become nil), `secretwrap` (makes secrets) and `dropsecretaccess` exist. A function's `secret.returns` is `always` (18 retail functions, for example `GetRaidTargetIndex`, `UnitSpellTargetName`, `UnitHealth`), `when` plus `secret.when` (184, for example `UnitHealthMax` under `UnitHealthMaxRestricted`, `UnitPower` under `UnitPowerRestricted`, `UnitGUID` and `UnitGroupRolesAssigned` under `UnitIdentityRestricted`, `C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDataByIndex` under `UnitAuraRestricted`, `UnitCastingInfo` under `UnitSpellCastRestricted`, `UnitThreatSituation` under `UnitThreatStateRestricted`) or `never` (4667). Tainted code cannot do arithmetic, comparison, boolean tests on boolean secrets, indexing, assignment, `#` or calls on a secret; it can concatenate strings and numbers, pass a secret to a Lua function, test a non-boolean secret for truthiness and call `type()` for the real type; a disallowed operation raises an immediate Lua error whose message text is not given | confirmed (the function names and meanings, the `always`/`when`/`never` fields and counts, the predicate meanings, the allowed and blocked operations); assumed (the exact error message text, and which restrictions are active in a given kit state: no source gives them) | `framescriptdocumentation.lua:40` (`canaccesssecrets`: "Returns true if the immediate calling function has appropriate permissions to access or operate on secret values."), `:151` (`dropsecretaccess`), `:257` and `:291` (`hasanysecretvalues`, `issecretvalue`: "Returns true if a supplied value is a secret value."), `:378` (`scrubsecretvalues`: "Returns a transformed list of values with inputs that are secret values replaced by nil values."), `:430` (`secretwrap`: "Converts all supplied values to secret values, preventing most operations on them from occurring on tainted code paths."); `secretpredicatesdocumentation.lua:112` (spell cast), `:132` (identity), `:137` (aura), `:142` (power); `secretpredicateapidocumentation.lua:95` (`HasSecretRestrictions`: "Returns true if this client build has secret value restrictions enabled. If false, all APIs that are tagged as potentially returning secrets will never do so."); wiki `Secret_Values`: "Tainted code is not allowed to perform arithmetic on secret values.", "Tainted code is allowed to concatenate secret values that are strings or numbers."; the counts come from `Manifest.load("retail").functions[...].secret`. Kit (`combat_secret.lua`, `combat_secret_value.lua`): a secret is a `newproxy` userdata; arithmetic, comparison, `#`, indexing, assignment and calls raise "attempt to perform <op> on a secret <type> value" for addon and unset contexts, concatenation of strings and numbers gives a secret string, and the engine ("blizzard", "scenario") context sees the real value; a secret passed to a kit API implementation or a `w:stub` reaches it as the value it hides, except for the readers that test, wrap or scrub a secret (`issecretvalue`, `canaccessvalue`, `hasanysecretvalues` and their siblings). Known gap: the manifest's `secret.args` (`AllowedWhenTainted`, `AllowedWhenUntainted`, `NotAllowed`) is not enforced, so addon code may pass a secret to any function. Revealed secret arguments are not re-hidden in results; functions whose manifest `secret.returns` is `never` return plain values (e.g. `AbbreviateNumbers`). Known gap: `type(secret)` returns `"userdata"` where the client returns the hidden value's type (argument validation sees the real type). Known gap: the kit never makes a boolean secret where the client does: a secret is a userdata, which every `if` takes as true, and Lua 5.1 cannot raise on a truth test, so addon code gets the plain boolean (a boolean in the combat log payload too). Known gap: a comparison of a secret with a plain number raises Lua 5.1's "attempt to compare userdata with number" (also in the engine context), because Lua 5.1 refuses mixed-type comparisons before any metamethod. Known gap: `string.format`, `tostring` and `GetStringWidth` do not model a secret argument. Known gap: secrecy ignores which unit a function reads (the player's own health is secret too) and the event `secretWhen`/`secretPayloads` fields are not modelled; `C_Secrets` predicates other than `HasSecretRestrictions` are true while Combat, Encounter, ChallengeMode or PvPMatch is active, whatever their arguments |
| C17 `C_CombatLog.IsCombatLogRestricted()` returns a boolean `restricted` and is never secret (present on all five flavors). What the combat log delivers while it is true is not stated: on retail 12.0.0 the unfiltered event is refused outright (C1), and Blizzard's own parser reads entries only through `C_CombatLogSecure.GetCurrentEventInfo` | confirmed (the signature, and that retail lists it among the functions added in 12.0.0); assumed (what is delivered while restricted, and when the flag is true: the wiki page has no description and the patch notes list the function with none) | `CombatLog.lua:105` (`IsCombatLogRestricted`, returns `restricted`, `secret.returns = "never"`), `blizzard_combatlogprocessor.lua:105` (`C_CombatLogSecure.GetCurrentEventInfo()`); wiki `API_C_CombatLog.IsCombatLogRestricted`: "restricted = C_CombatLog.IsCombatLogRestricted()", no description; wiki `Patch_12.0.0/API_changes` lists it among the added global functions |
| C18 Flavor differences in the API data (`Manifest.load` over retail, vanilla, forever, mists, tbc). Missing on every flavor, so installed only by the deprecation fallbacks (G1): bare `CombatLogGetCurrentEventInfo`, `UnitAura`, `UnitBuff`, `UnitDebuff`. `C_CombatLog.GetCurrentEventInfo` exists on vanilla, mists and tbc and not on retail or forever (the wiki says `CombatLogGetCurrentEventInfo` is "deprecated in patch 12.0.0" and defined in `Deprecated_CombatLog.lua`, which assigns it from that missing function, so it is nil on retail and forever). Present on all five: `C_CombatLogSecure.GetCurrentEventInfo`, `C_CombatLog.IsCombatLogRestricted`, `UnitGroupRolesAssigned`, `UnitThreatSituation`, `UnitDetailedThreatSituation`, `C_NamePlate.GetNamePlateForUnit`, `GetNamePlates`, `GetNamePlateSize`, `SetNamePlateSize`, `C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDataByIndex`, `GetAuraDataByAuraInstanceID`, `GetUnitAuras`, `UnitCastingInfo`, `UnitChannelInfo`, `UnitGUID`, `issecretvalue`, `C_Secrets.ShouldAurasBeSecret`, `C_RestrictedActions.IsAddOnRestrictionActive`, `InCombatLockdown`, `GetRaidRosterInfo` and `GetNumGroupMembers` (names in `globals.api` with no signature), and `UnitAffectingCombat` (a signature on retail and forever, a name only on vanilla, mists and tbc). The data lists a function on a flavor when that flavor's documentation does, so presence is not proof that the flavor's client behaves like retail's | confirmed (the diff, as data) | `Manifest.load(<flavor>).functions` and `globals.api` for the five flavors, run 2026-10-27 with `tools/run51.py --script`; `vanilla/CombatLog.lua:79`, `mists/CombatLog.lua:79`, `tbc/CombatLog.lua:79` (`C_CombatLog.GetCurrentEventInfo`); `blizzard_deprecatedcombatlog/deprecated_combatlog.lua:18` (`CombatLogGetCurrentEventInfo = C_CombatLog.GetCurrentEventInfo`); wiki `API_CombatLogGetCurrentEventInfo`: "This function was deprecated in patch 12.0.0" |

## Taint

Wiki pages read 2026-10-27 (`warcraft.wiki.gg`, fetched through a summarising reader, so a quoted sentence is secondhand): `Secure_Execution_and_Tainting`, `API_issecurevariable`, `API_securecall`, `API_secureexecuterange`, `API_forceinsecure`, `API_hooksecurefunc`, `ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED`, `ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN`, `API_ScriptRegion_IsProtected`, `API_ScriptRegion_CanChangeProtectedState`, `API_FrameScriptObject_IsForbidden`, `API_Frame_GetAttribute`, `SecureActionButtonTemplate`, `SecureHandlerBaseTemplate`, `SecureStateDriver`, `Macro_conditionals`, `Secure_command_options`, `API_SecureCmdOptionParse`, `CVar_taintLog`, `Secret_Values`, and the category `Category:API_functions/restricted`. `Protected_functions`, `Restricted_environment`, `Category:API_functions/protected` and `Category:API_functions/nocombat` returned 404 and are not sources; the protected and combat-only lists in TS8 come from the `restrictedapi` template at the top of each page in `Category:API_functions/restricted`, read through the wiki's `api.php` (lead section only, again through the summariser, so each label is secondhand). TS10's frame-name evidence is one forum thread read in full and three GitHub issue titles seen only in search results (their pages were not opened). Blizzard citations are `extract retail 70575 <path>:<line>` with `<path>` relative to `code/addons/` in the local client extract (never committed), or a path under `mirror/gethe/<branch>/Interface/AddOns/` for the classic flavours at the commits in the table at the top; signatures and flags from `api/<flavor>/` (a path without a flavor is `api/retail/`, version 12.1.0.69933, which is older than the extract). The classic mirrors hold `Blizzard_FrameXML/SecureTemplates.lua` and `Blizzard_RestrictedAddOnEnvironment/*.lua` with the same functions as retail (checked for `SecureHandlers.lua` `:267`, `:435-438`, `:497`, `:506` and `SecureStateDriver.lua:63`, identical to retail); `UIParent.lua` exists only under `Blizzard_UIParent/<Era>/` in the classic flavours; `forever` has no `ADDON_ACTION` handler Lua in its mirror, only the docs file.

The kit has these known gaps, which may not be the only ones:

- TS4: a read of a tainted slot taints the execution only where kit code calls `Taint.read` (the dropdown globals, the tooltip unit APIs, and the globals secure kit code calls: snippet forwards, action-button actions, macro-conditional and unit-watch unit queries, plus the functions in the `C_` namespaces those reach, which are declared when the world installs); a read of an undeclared table or of any other global does not taint.
- A direct call from one addon's function into another addon's function keeps the first addon's taint; the client taints by the data and code it reads, the kit by the `callAsAddon` entry.
- Taint is per `callAsAddon` or `World:run` entry, not per statement: code that reads a tainted value mid-function stays under its own owner's name, not the tainted slot's.
- TS8: the protected and combat-only lists are the wiki's, not the engine's; a function missing from them is not blocked, and the kit has no list of hardware-event or `noscript` functions.
- TS1, TS2: a macro or `/run` script taints with the empty string in the client (`API_issecurevariable`); the kit gives that taint only to the session's `/run`, `/script` and `/dump` chunks and the functions they define (it runs no macros), and its log text names it "a macro".

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| TS1 Execution is tainted by the addon whose code runs, until that entry returns; `issecure()` is false while it is, true for Blizzard code. Code that reads data or code an addon introduced is tainted too (TS4); a `loadstring` chunk and the functions it defines run with the taint of the code that built it (none for secure code or `forceinsecure`). Kit limit: those functions are credited by the chunk's source key (its chunk name, or its text when unnamed), so chunks with the same name, or the same text when unnamed, share the latest builder's taint for the functions they define; each top-level chunk keeps its own | confirmed (Blizzard code branches on `issecure()` as the secure-versus-addon test); assumed (that an addon entry taints from its first statement and stops at its return: the engine does this, no Lua shows it; that a built chunk keeps its builder's taint) | `blizzard_chatframebase/shared/slashcommandsregistry.lua:44` and `:54` (`issecure`, the secure and the addon registration paths), `blizzard_uiparentpanelmanager/shared/uiparentpanelmanager.lua:845` (`issecure` tested next to `InCombatLockdown`), `blizzard_restrictedaddonenvironment/restrictedexecution.lua:469-470` (`CallRestrictedClosure` refuses insecure callers); wiki `Secure_Execution_and_Tainting`: "execution becomes 'tainted' as soon as it reads data or code introduced by a third party."; wiki `API_securecall`: the path "will remain secure when `securecall` returns" |
| TS2 `issecurevariable([tbl,] name)` returns `isSecure, taintingAddon`: secure is `true, nil` (also for a key never used); tainted is `false, "<addon>"` (an empty string when a macro tainted it); `tbl` defaults to `_G`; a number is turned into a string, any other type of key is an error; when `tbl[name]` is nil and `tbl` has a metatable with `__index`, the answer is for `__index[name]` | assumed | no source in Lua (`issecurevariable` appears in no Blizzard addon file in the extract); wiki `API_issecurevariable`: "isSecure, taint = issecurevariable([tbl,] variable)", "Also returns `true, nil` for keys that have never been used/defined, because nothing has tainted them yet.", "taint: string? - name of the addon that tainted the table field; an empty string if tainted by a macro; nil if secure." |

Explicit means the frame was created protected, which is what `SecureFrameTemplate` (`protected="true"`) and every template that inherits it do. | TS14 SecureActionButton. **Attribute lookup:** `SecureButton_GetModifiedAttribute(frame, name, button, prefix, suffix)` takes `prefix` from `SecureButton_GetModifierPrefix(frame)` (the frame's `modifiers` attribute when set, else `alt-` then `ctrl-` then `shift-` for the keys held, so `alt-ctrl-shift-` in that order) and `suffix` from `SecureButton_GetButtonSuffix(button)` (`LeftButton` 1, `RightButton` 2, `MiddleButton` 3, `Button4` to `Button31` 4 to 31, any other name `-<name>`, no button an empty string), then asks the engine's `frame:GetAttribute(prefix, name, suffix)`, which tries `prefix..name..suffix`, `"*"..name..suffix`, `prefix..name.."*"`, `"*"..name.."*"`, then `name`. When that finds nothing and the frame has `useparent-<name>` or `useparent*`, it repeats on the parent; an empty-string value counts as nil. **Click path:** `SecureActionButton_OnClick(self, button, down, isKeyPress, isSecureAction)`: a hardware mouse click (`isSecureAction` and not `isKeyPress`) acts on the up event; any other call, including an addon's `Click()`, acts on the down event when the `useOnKeyDown` attribute or the `ActionButtonUseKeyDown` CVar says so, else on up, and returns true when it acted. Then `GetConvertedButtonUnitAndActionType`: `downbutton` remap on a down press, the unit from `unit` plus `unitsuffix` (with pet and vehicle remaps, `checkselfcast`, `checkfocuscast`, `checkmouseovercast`), `harmbutton` or `helpbutton` remap by `UnitCanAttack` or `UnitCanAssist`, **no action when the unit does not exist** (unless `none`), then the `type` attribute (`typerelease` on a hold release). **Action types and what each calls:** `spell` CastSpellByID(number) or CastSpellByName(name), both with `unit`; `item` C_Item.EquipItemByName when equippable and not worn, else SecureCmdUseItem(name, bag, slot, unit); `macro` RunMacro(macro) or C_Macro.RunMacroText(macrotext); `target` ClearTarget (`none`), SpellTargetUnit (while targeting), C_Item.DropItemOnUnit (cursor item) or TargetUnit; `focus` FocusUnit; `assist` AssistUnit; `stop` SpellStopTargeting; `cancelaura` CancelSpellByName, or C_PaperDollInfo.CancelTemporaryEnchantment, or CancelUnitBuff; `pet` CastPetAction; `toy` UseToy or UseToyByName; `click` `clickbutton:Click(button)`; `attribute` `frame:SetAttribute(name, value)` from `attribute-frame`, `attribute-name`, `attribute-value`; `action` UseAction; `togglemenu` and `menu` open a unit menu; also `actionbar`, `actionrelease`, `flyout`, `multispell`, `equipmentset`, `leavevehicle`, `destroytotem`, `maintank`, `mainassist`, `raidtarget`, `worldmarker`, `teleporthome`, `returnhome`, `visithouse`, `outfit`. Any other `type` runs `_<type>` or `rawget(self, type)` after `forceinsecure()` (a function is called with `(self, unit, button, isKeyPress, down)`; a string runs as `SecureHandler_OnClick`). **Templates:** `SecureActionButtonTemplate` is `SecureFrameTemplate` plus `SecureActionButtonMixin` and `OnClick = SecureActionButton_OnClick`; `SecureUnitButtonTemplate` is `SecureFrameTemplate` plus `OnClick = SecureUnitButton_OnClick` and nothing else: the defaults `*type1 = target`, `*type2 = menu`, `unit`, `menu-function` and `RegisterForClicks("AnyUp")` are set by the separate function `SecureUnitButton_OnLoad`, which the template does not call | confirmed (all of the above except the engine's `GetAttribute` order, the engine's `Click()` argument defaults and the CVar's default); assumed (the `GetAttribute(prefix, name, suffix)` order: wiki `API_Frame_GetAttribute` states it, the engine implements it; the `ActionButtonUseKeyDown` default and what `Click()` passes as `down`) | `blizzard_framexml/securetemplates.lua:70-92`, `:101-117`, `:119-137` (lookup, `useparent`), `:143-199` (`SecureButton_GetModifiedUnit`), `:201-212`, `:262-584` (the `SECURE_ACTIONS.<type>` table; `spell` `:393-402`, `item` `:415-436`, `macro` `:447-462`, `target` `:509-522`, `focus` `:524`, `assist` `:529`), `:694-731`, `:733-760` (`PerformAction`, `forceinsecure` `:752`), `:795-828`, `:831-842` (`SecureUnitButton_OnLoad`), `:844-889`; `blizzard_framexml/securetemplates.xml:4-25`; wiki `API_Frame_GetAttribute`: "this function attempts to return attributes in the following order"; classic_era `Blizzard_FrameXML/SecureTemplates.lua:113`, `:503`, `:799`, `:825` (same functions, six lines earlier) |
| TS15 SecureHandler templates. Each runs the attribute named as a snippet through `SecureHandler_Self_Execute(self, signature, body, ...)` in the frame's own restricted environment: `SecureHandlerBaseTemplate` none (`OnLoad` adds the methods below; size 2x2); `...StateTemplate` `_onstate-<id>` on a change of attribute `state-<id>` (`self,stateid,newstate`); `...AttributeTemplate` `_onattributechanged` on any attribute change whose name does not start with `_` (`self,name,value`); `...ClickTemplate` `_onclick` (`self,button,down`); `...DoubleClickTemplate` `_ondoubleclick` (`self,button`); `...DragTemplate` `_ondragstart` (`self,button,kind,value,...`, its return values go to the cursor pickup) and `_onreceivedrag`; `...MouseUpDownTemplate` `_onmouseup` and `_onmousedown` (`self,button`); `...MouseWheelTemplate` `_onmousewheel` (`self,delta`); `...EnterLeaveTemplate` `_onenter` and `_onleave` (`self`); `...ShowHideTemplate` `_onshow` and `_onhide` (`self`). **Methods on handler frames:** `Execute(body)`, `SetFrameRef(label, frame)` (reference read back with the handle method `GetFrameRef(label)`, attribute `frameref-<label>`), `WrapScript(frame, script, preBody[, postBody])` (the frame it is called on is the header; the global is `SecureHandlerWrapScript(frame, script, header, preBody, postBody)`), `UnwrapScript(frame, script)` returns `header, preBody, postBody`. All four go through attributes set on one protected frame, and **all raise "Cannot use SecureHandlers API during combat" in combat for every caller, secure or not**; they also require the header to be explicitly protected and not forbidden. `CallRestrictedClosure` itself raises "Cannot call restricted closure from insecure code" when `issecure()` is false, so a snippet run from tainted execution errors rather than runs. **Wrap:** the wrapper replaces the frame's script (so a later `SetScript` replaces the wrap and `UnwrapScript` then finds none); supported scripts are OnClick, OnDoubleClick, PreClick, PostClick, OnEnter, OnLeave, OnShow, OnHide, OnDragStart, OnReceiveDrag, OnMouseWheel, OnAttributeChanged. The pre snippet runs only when `not InCombatLockdown() or frame:IsProtected()`; for the click scripts it gets `(self, button, down)` and returns `newbutton, message`: `false` skips the original handler and the post snippet, any other non-nil first return replaces the button (as a string); the original handler then runs through `securecall` (an error goes to the error handler); the post snippet runs **only when the pre snippet returned a non-nil message**, with `(self, message, button, down)`. OnShow/OnHide: `self` then `self,message`; OnMouseWheel: `self,offset` then `self,message,offset`; OnEnter/OnLeave: `self` then `self,message`, only when `motion` is true (the pre snippet of OnLeave only after an OnEnter that set `_wrapentered`); OnAttributeChanged: `self,name,value` then `self,message,name,value`, for names not starting `_`; OnDragStart/OnReceiveDrag: the pre snippet gets `self,button,kind,value,...` and its first return is `false` (skip), `"message"` plus the message, or a cursor kind that is picked up. **Restricted environment:** snippet text may contain neither the word `function` nor `{` or `}`; the environment holds `self` (a frame handle), `control` (the header's handle), `math`, `string`, `select`, `tonumber`, `tostring`, `rawtype`, the string and math functions by their global names (format, gmatch, gsub, strbyte, strchar, strfind, strjoin, strlen, strlower, strmatch, strrep, strrev, strsplit, strsub, strtrim, strupper, abs, ceil, floor, max, min, mod, random, sin, cos and the rest), the restricted-table functions (newtable, copytable, pairs, ipairs, next, unpack, wipe, tinsert, tremove, type, rtgsub, `table.{maxn,insert,remove,sort,concat,wipe,new}`), the macro-conditional queries (SecureCmdOptionParse, GetShapeshiftForm, IsStealthed, UnitExists, UnitIsDead, UnitIsGhost, UnitPlayerOrPetInParty, UnitPlayerOrPetInRaid, the Is...KeyDown family, IsModifiedClick, GetMouseButtonClicked, IsMounted, IsSwimming, IsSubmerged, IsFlying, IsFlyableArea, IsAdvancedFlyableArea, IsDrivableArea, IsIndoors, IsOutdoors, CanExitVehicle), GetActionBarPage, GetBindingKey, GetBonusBarIndex, GetBonusBarOffset, GetOverrideBarIndex, GetTempShapeshiftBarIndex, GetVehicleBarIndex, HasAction, the Has...ActionBar family, IsHarmfulItem, IsHelpfulItem, IsSpellHarmful, IsSpellHelpful, FindSpellBookSlotBySpellID, PlayerCanAttack, PlayerCanAssist, PlayerIsChanneling, PlayerPetSummary, PlayerInCombat, PlayerInGroup, UnitHasVehicleUI, GetActionInfo, GetTransmogOutfitIndex, IsGamePadEnabled, GetGamePadState, `print`, and Register/Unregister for StateDriver, AttributeDriver and UnitWatch plus UnitWatchRegistered. Handle methods: GetName, GetID, IsShown, IsVisible, GetWidth, GetHeight, GetScale, GetEffectiveScale, GetRect, GetFrameLevel, GetFrameStrata, the Is...Enabled queries, GetObjectType, IsObjectType, IsProtected, GetAttribute (a name starting `_` returns nothing), GetFrameRef, GetEffectiveAttribute, GetChildren, GetChildList, GetParent, GetMousePosition, IsUnderMouse, GetNumPoints, GetPoint, Show, Hide, SetID, SetWidth, SetHeight, SetScale, SetAlpha, ClearAllPoints, SetPoint, SetAllPoints, SetAttribute, the SetBinding family and ClearBindings, Raise, Lower, SetFrameLevel, SetFrameStrata, SetParent, the Enable...Mouse, Keyboard and GamePad calls, RegisterAutoHide, UnregisterAutoHide, AddToAutoHide, Enable, Disable, Run, RunFor, RunAttribute, ChildUpdate, CallMethod | confirmed (all of the above, from the Lua) | `blizzard_restrictedaddonenvironment/securehandlertemplates.xml:6-105`, `securehandlers.lua:79-166` (the `SecureHandler_On...` functions), `:267-269`, `:272-290` (`Wrapped_Click`), `:293-380` (the other wrappers), `:396-409`, `:430-440` (combat error `:435-438`), `:460-530` (`_wrap` and `_unwrap` branches, "Header frame must be explicitly protected" `:497`, "Wrap frame cannot be used" `:506`), `:602-760` (the global API and `SecureHandler_OnLoad` `:752-757`), `restrictedenvironment.lua:24-78` and `:81-92` and `:139-265` (the ENV table), `restrictedexecution.lua:58-70` (`function` and `{`/`}` refused), `:276-331` (restricted globals), `:444-482` (`CallRestrictedClosure`, insecure refusal `:469-470`), `restrictedframes.lua:104-830` (the `HANDLE` methods); wiki `SecureHandlerBaseTemplate` (template and attribute names; "`WrapScript`: Wraps a widget script with code snippets executed in the restricted environment."; no combat or return-value text); wiki `Restricted_environment` returned 404 |
| TS16 `RegisterStateDriver(frame, state, conditional)` is `RegisterAttributeDriver(frame, "state-"..state, conditional)`; an attribute name starting `_` is refused. The manager frame (a hidden protected frame) re-evaluates `SecureCmdOptionParse(conditional)` for every registered frame from `OnUpdate` no more often than every 0.2 s (`STATE_DRIVER_UPDATE_THROTTLE`, changed by the manager's `updatetime` attribute), and these events reset the timer so the next frame rescans: `MODIFIER_STATE_CHANGED`, `ACTIONBAR_PAGE_CHANGED`, `UPDATE_BONUS_ACTIONBAR`, `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`, `UPDATE_SHAPESHIFT_FORM`, `UPDATE_STEALTH`, `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED`, `PLAYER_FOCUS_CHANGED`, `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`, `PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED`, `UNIT_PET` and `GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE`; registering evaluates once immediately, and registering an empty conditional removes that driver. A result is set on the frame as the attribute only if it differs from the current value (a numeric string becomes a number, the string `nil` clears it, no result changes nothing). The attribute `state-visibility` is special: result `show` shows the frame and clears `statehidden`, `hide` hides it and sets `statehidden`. `RegisterUnitWatch(frame, asState)` watches the frame's `unit` (`SecureButton_GetUnit`) on the same throttle: with `asState` it sets `state-unitexists` to true or false, else it shows or hides the frame (and sets `statehidden`); `UnregisterUnitWatch` and `UnitWatchRegistered` exist; `UnregisterAttributeDriver(frame)` with no attribute removes every driver of the frame | confirmed | `blizzard_restrictedaddonenvironment/securestatedriver.lua:8-49` (the API), `:63` (throttle), `:69-88` (unit watch), `:95-117` (`resolveDriver`, `state-visibility`), `:119-139` (`OnUpdate`), `:141-177` (`OnEvent`, `OnAttributeChanged`), `:179-196` (manager frame, events); wiki `SecureStateDriver`: "no more often than STATE_DRIVER_UPDATE_THROTTLE", "currently 0.2 sec." |
| TS17 Macro-conditional options: clauses are separated by `;`; each clause is a run of bracketed conditions followed by a value; commas inside one bracket group are AND; the first satisfied condition wins and its value is returned, no value when none is; an empty `[]` is always satisfied; `no` before a conditional reverses it; `@unit` and `target=unit` set the clause's target; `mod:shift`, `group:raid`, `combat`, `harm`, `help`, `exists`, `dead` are conditionals. `SecureCmdOptionParse(options)` returns `result, target` (nil when nothing matches). ## Blizzard UI

The opt-in layer that loads Blizzard's own UI addons from the local extract (`blizzard_ui.lua`, Ruling R7). Sources are the extract's files (retail build 70575, read 2026-10-28); the client's loader is C, so how it reads these TOC forms is inferred from the files. Tests: `tests/test_blizzard_ui.lua`, `tests/test_toc.lua`.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| BU1 the default key bindings come from `Blizzard_FrameXML/Bindings_Standard.xml` on retail and `Bindings_Mists.xml` on mists; other flavors get the note "no default Bindings.xml" | assumed | the extract's `addons/blizzard_framexml` holds `bindings_standard.xml`, `bindings_mists.xml`, `bindings_wowhack.xml` and `bindings_wowlabs.xml`, none listed in `blizzard_framexml.toc`, and no `bindings.xml`; the file per game type is read from the names, not from loader code |
| BU2 a dependency line's `[AllowLoadGameType ...]` drops the dependency on flavors outside the list, and `[AllowLoad Glue]` (a list without `game`) drops it in the game UI | assumed | `blizzard_actionbar.toc` lists `## Dep: Blizzard_StoreUI [AllowLoadGameType mainline]` and `## Dep: Blizzard_FrameXML [AllowLoadGameType classic]`; `blizzard_storeui.toc` and six others list `[AllowLoad Glue]` dependencies such as `Blizzard_GlueParent`; the same bracket form on file lines is TOC_format's game-type filter. A list naming no known game type does not drop the dependency, as on file lines |
| BU3 `[Family]` in a TOC file line is the flavor's family (its last TOC suffix: `Mainline`, `Classic`) and `[Game]` its game type (its first TOC suffix: `Standard`, `Camelot`, `Mists`, `Vanilla`, `TBC`), for every addon, not only Blizzard's; another `[Variable]` (`[TextLocale]`) is left as written | confirmed (both variables and their values for every kit flavor); assumed (`Camelot` on forever, which the wiki says "may be changed to Forever in the future") | wiki `TOC_format` (read 2026-10-29), "Variables": "TOC files support the use of variable expansions for files", `Family` "Expands to a game type family (e.g., Mainline, or Classic)", `Game` "Expands to a game type (e.g., Standard, Vanilla, Camelot, TBC, Wrath, or Mists)", added in 11.1.5. A timer scheduled outside the timers keeps `due = now + seconds`, so `C_Timer.After(0, f)` then `advance(0)` fires `f` once. ## Blizzard data

Wiki pages are on warcraft.wiki.gg, read 2026-10-28. Blizzard window code is not in the mirror (it holds no window addon), so it is read on GitHub `Gethe/wow-ui-source` at each branch's pinned `COMMIT` (live `b9be937`, forever `172a387`, classic = mists `15c8a75`, classic_era = vanilla `8165d4c`, classic_anniversary = tbc `2cfec7c`), as `<commit>:Interface/AddOns/<Addon>/<file>`; paths marked "extract retail 70575" are in the local client extract. Only retail 70575 has an extract; tbc 2.5.6.70577 and forever 1.60.1.70658 are installed but not extracted, vanilla and mists have neither. tbc stands in for vanilla. A GitHub 404 is not a source and a row says so where one was hit. The retail extract holds none of the 16 window DB2 tables.

| Rule | Verdict | Source |
|---|---|---|
| B1 each window's addon folder and whether its TOC says `## LoadOnDemand: 1` (LoD). Retail and forever: talents `Blizzard_PlayerSpells` (LoD 1; Dependencies `Blizzard_SharedTalentUI`, `Blizzard_PagedContent`, `Blizzard_HelpPlate`) plus `Blizzard_SharedTalentUI` (LoD 0, so it loads at startup with `Blizzard_SpellSearch`); map `Blizzard_WorldMap_Mainline.toc` (no LoD line); merchant in `Blizzard_UIPanels_Game` (no LoD; its mainline TOC lists the LoD addon `Blizzard_TransmogShared` under Dependencies, so that loads at startup too); mail `Blizzard_MailFrame` (no LoD; retail and forever only); auction `Blizzard_AuctionHouseUI` (LoD 1); transmog `Blizzard_Transmog`, `Blizzard_Collections` and `Blizzard_TransmogShared` (all LoD 1). Forever has no `Blizzard_TalentUI`. tbc and vanilla: talents `Blizzard_TalentUI_TBC.toc` / `_Vanilla.toc` (LoD 1, Dependency `Blizzard_HelpPlate`); map `Blizzard_WorldMap_TBC.toc` / `_Vanilla.toc` (LoD 0); merchant and mail inside `Blizzard_UIPanels_Game_Classic.toc` (no LoD, `LoadFirst: 1`; merchant `TBC\MerchantFrame.*` or `Vanilla\MerchantFrame.*`, mail `Classic\MailFrame.*`; there is no `Blizzard_MailFrame` or `Blizzard_PlayerSpells` on the classic family); auction `Blizzard_AuctionUI_Classic.toc` (LoD 1, `AllowLoadGameType: classic`). Mists: `Blizzard_TalentUI_Mists.toc` (LoD 1), `Blizzard_WorldMap_Mists.toc` (no LoD line), both `Blizzard_AuctionUI_Classic.toc` and `Blizzard_AuctionHouseUI_Classic.toc` (LoD 1), `Blizzard_Collections_Mists.toc` (LoD 1). tbc and vanilla have no `Blizzard_Collections` TOC (only Cata, Mainline, Mists, Wrath), so no transmog window loads there. `Blizzard_AuctionHouseUI_Classic.toc` lists `AllowLoadGameType: cata, mists` at the tbc pin `2cfec7c` but `tbc, wrath, cata, mists` at the mists pin `15c8a75`, so the tbc auction family at 2.5.6.70577 is not settled; `Blizzard_FrameEffects` (`blizzard_frameeffects.toc` has no LoD line; `Blizzard_ClassTalentsFrame.lua:1302` calls its `GlowEmitterFactory`) loads at startup on retail and forever, so it is in `SHARED` | confirmed (retail from the extract, the rest from the pinned TOCs); assumed (the tbc auction family at the installed build; that the classic client uses `Blizzard_Transmog`) | extract retail 70575 `code/addons/blizzard_playerspells/blizzard_playerspells.toc:3`, `blizzard_sharedtalentui/*.toc` (`LoadOnDemand: 0`, Dependency `Blizzard_SpellSearch`), `blizzard_worldmap/blizzard_worldmap_mainline.toc`, `blizzard_mailframe/*.toc`, `blizzard_uipanels_game/blizzard_uipanels_game_mainline.toc` and `_classic.toc`, `blizzard_auctionhouseui/*.toc`, `blizzard_transmog/*.toc`, `blizzard_transmogshared/*.toc`, `blizzard_collections/*.toc`, `blizzard_talentui/blizzard_talentui_mists.toc`. GitHub trees `classic_anniversary` / `classic_era` / `classic` `Interface/AddOns` (218 entries each) and the TOCs named above. No extract of tbc, forever, vanilla or mists: the classic-family and forever TOCs are GitHub reads at the pinned commits, not the installed builds. GitHub 404: `Blizzard_MailFrame/*` and `Blizzard_AuctionHouseUI/Blizzard_AuctionHouseUI.toc` at `2cfec7c` (no such files there; a 404 is not a source for the rule) |
| B2 how each window opens. Retail and forever: `PLAYER_INTERACTION_MANAGER_FRAME_SHOW(interactionType)` handled by `PlayerInteractionFrameManagerMixin:OnEvent` (`ShowFrame`), with `RegisterPlayerInteraction(Enum.PlayerInteractionType.<type>, ...)` for Merchant (5), MailInfo (17), Auctioneer (21), Transmogrifier (24); the same enum values hold on all five flavors. They do not open on `MERCHANT_SHOW`, `MAIL_SHOW` or `TRANSMOGRIFY_OPEN`. Retail auction also opens on `AUCTION_HOUSE_SHOW` (`GameEvent.HandleAuctionHouseShow`, then `ShowAuctionHouseFrame`). Talents: retail `TogglePlayerSpellsFrame(suggestedTab, inspectUnit)` / `ToggleClassTalentFrame`; mists, vanilla, tbc `ToggleTalentFrame` in `Blizzard_UIParent` (loads `Blizzard_TalentUI`, checks `C_SpecializationInfo.CanPlayerUseTalentUI()`, then `ShowUIPanel(PlayerTalentFrame)`). Map: `ToggleWorldMap`. `ShowUIPanel` / `HideUIPanel` are defined in `Blizzard_UIParentPanelManager`. Classic family: `TRANSMOGRIFY_OPEN` loads Collections and shows `WardrobeFrame` (mists, vanilla and tbc UIParent); `AUCTION_HOUSE_SHOW` picks the legacy or the modern auction window by `IsUsingLegacyAuctionClient()`; `MerchantFrame_MerchantShow()` and `MailFrame_Show()` are defined as globals, but no `MERCHANT_SHOW` / `MAIL_SHOW` handler or `RegisterPlayerInteraction` was found in those files, so the caller is engine side (inferred from the fetched files, not a grep over every addon) | confirmed (retail from the extract; classic family from the pinned UIParent); assumed (the classic merchant and mail caller) | extract retail 70575 `blizzard_uipanels_game/shared/playerinteractionframemanager.lua:114` (`ShowFrame`), `:170` (`OnEvent`), `blizzard_uipanels_game/mainline/merchantframe.lua:83-92`, `blizzard_mailframe/mailframe.lua:78-87`, `blizzard_auctionhouseui/shared/blizzard_auctionhouseui_bootstrap.lua`, `blizzard_transmog/blizzard_transmog_bootstrap.lua`, `blizzard_game/mainline/eventimplementation.lua:450` (`HandleAuctionHouseShow`), `blizzard_framexmlutil/mainline/playerspellsutil.lua:156`, `blizzard_worldmap/blizzard_worldmap.lua:576`, `blizzard_uiparentpanelmanager/shared/uiparentpanelmanager.lua:861` (`ShowUIPanel`), `:886`; mirror `classic_anniversary/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_UIParent/TBC/UIParent.lua:203`, `:218`, `:262`, `:948`, `classic/.../Mists/UIParent.lua:216`, `:239`, `:339`, `:1152`, `classic_era/.../Vanilla/UIParent.lua:198`, `:245`, `:940`. Classic merchant and mail files: GitHub `Blizzard_UIPanels_Game/TBC/MerchantFrame.lua`, `Classic/MailFrame.lua` at `2cfec7c` |
| B3 `C_AddOns.LoadAddOn(name)` returns `loaded, value`; `loaded` is true when the addon loaded or was already loaded, so a second call returns true. That it loads the addon's dependencies, fires `ADDON_LOADED` and loads nothing the second time is not on the page | confirmed (returns true on a second call); assumed (dependencies are loaded, `ADDON_LOADED` fires once, nothing reloads) | wiki `API_C_AddOns.LoadAddOn`: "true if the addon was loaded successfully, or if it has already been loaded"; the page is silent on dependencies and on repeat calls beyond that. Blizzard's own TOC `Dependencies` lines (B1) and `UIParentLoadAddOn` (mirror `classic_anniversary/Interface/AddOns/Blizzard_UIParent/Shared/UIParent.lua:250-258`) rely on `LoadAddOn` loading a window with its dependencies |
| B4 vanilla and tbc talent trees: `GetNumTalentTabs([isInspect])` returns `numTabs`; `GetNumTalents(tabIndex)` returns `numTalents`; `GetTalentTabInfo(tabIndex, isInspect, isPet, groupIndex)` returns `id, name, description, icon, pointsSpent, background, previewPointsSpent, isUnlocked`; `GetTalentInfo(tabIndex, talentIndex, isInspect, isPet, groupIndex)` returns `name, icon, tier, column, rank, maxRank, meetsPrereq, previewRank, meetsPreviewPrereq, isExceptional, hasGoldBorder, talentID` (not `isExceptional, available` after `maxRank`); `GetTalentPrereqs(tabIndex, talentIndex)` returns `tier, column, isLearnable`; `UnitCharacterPoints("player")` returns the unspent points. `GetTalentTabInfo` and `GetTalentInfo` are Lua fallbacks in `Blizzard_DeprecatedSpecialization` (installed only while the `loadDeprecationFallbacks` CVar is true, default 1, G1; the C versions went away after tbc 2.5.5, vanilla 1.15.8, mists 5.5.0), so no `api/` data has them; the same file on mists has a different shape (`GetTalentTabInfo` returns `id, name, description, icon`, `GetTalentInfo` builds `{tier, column, groupIndex, isInspect, target}`). The tbc, vanilla and mists windows themselves call `C_SpecializationInfo.GetTalentInfo(query)`, `C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecializationInfo`, `GetNumTalentTabs(inspect, pet)`, `GetUnspentTalentPoints`, `GetNumTalentGroups` and `TalentFrame_Update`, not the legacy names. `GetNumTalentTabs`, `GetNumTalents`, `GetTalentPrereqs`, `GetUnspentTalentPoints`, `UnitCharacterPoints`, `ToggleTalentFrame` are in the mists, vanilla and tbc data only; `LearnTalent` in all five; `GetGroupPreviewTalentPointsSpent(isPet, group)` is the sum of every talent's `previewRank` (assumed; `talentframebase.lua:302` subtracts it from the unspent points) and `Constants.TalentConsts.NumTalentColumns` is 4 on vanilla and tbc, 3 on mists (assumed; the generated API data holds 0 and `TalentFrame_Load` loops it) | confirmed (return lists: the fallback Lua and the wiki agree; flavor lists: a name scan of `api/*/*.lua` and `globals.lua`); assumed (that the fallbacks are present in a stock client: the CVar default is G1, no source for the vanilla build) | extract retail 70575 `blizzard_deprecatedspecialization/deprecated_specialization_tbc.lua` (identical to `_vanilla.lua`; guard `GetCVarBool("loadDeprecationFallbacks")` at line 4), `deprecated_specialization_mists.lua`, `blizzard_deprecatedspecialization.toc` (`AllowLoadGameType: classic, standard`), `blizzard_talentui/mists/blizzard_talentui.lua:202`, `:710`, `blizzard_framexml/mists/talentframebase.lua:71`; wiki `API_GetTalentTabInfo`, `API_GetTalentInfo`, `API_GetNumTalentTabs`, `API_GetNumTalents`, `API_GetTalentPrereqs`, `API_UnitCharacterPoints`; `api/{tbc,vanilla,mists}/SpecializationInfo.lua`.
